6. nariyippariyākkiyatu
aṭikaḷ poruṭṭē
iṉi, aracaṉiyuḷḷiṭ ṭellārun tattam irukki cērntapiṉ pakalavaṉum mēlpāl maṟintaṉaṉ. kutirittuṟik kāvalarum ak kutirikaḷip pantikaḷil niralpaṭak kaṭṭi, avi tamakku uṇavākap paruppum neyyuṅ karuppuk kaṭṭiyum cērttuk kalantu, iṉiya pullik koṇarntu iṭṭup, payaṟuṅ koḷḷuṅ kaṭaliyun tuvariyuṅ kaḻuvi vēvuvittup piyil niṟiyap peytu kaṭṭi evvaḷavō muyaṉṟa tīṉi koṭuttum, avikaḷ avaṟṟi ayiṉṟila. piṉṉar varavara iruḷmikuntu naṭuyāmam ātalum, vanta ap putupparikaḷellām narikaḷāka urumāṟi, appantikaḷil tamakku muṉṉarē yirunta kutirikaḷiṉ aṭivayiṟṟik kaṭittu uḷḷuḷḷa kuṭarkaḷip piṭuṅku vaṉavum, kaḻuttik kaṭittu irattatti māntuvaṉavum, uyirtuṟantu kīḻvīḻnta paḻaṅ kutirikaḷiṉ uṭampik kiḻittu uḷḷuḷḷa taci niṇam mūḷi mutaliyavaṟṟik kiṇṭik kiḷaṟit tiṉpaṉavumāyp pantiyilirunta paḻaṅ kutirikaḷiyellām orumikkak koṉṟu, piṟaku avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭut terukkaḷilum, āvaṇaṅkaḷilum, aṅ
kaṇaṅkaḷilum, vīṭukaḷ maṭaṅkaḷ cāvaṭikaḷeṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ muṟṟaṅkaḷilun tiraḷ tiraḷāyc ceṉṟu ūḷiyiṭṭu ōṭuvavāyiṉa. in nikaḻcciyik kaṇṭu verukkoṇṭa parittuṟik kāvalarum nakark kāvalarum avaṟṟi aṭittuk kollutaṟkup paṭik kalaṅkaḷōṭu etirttum, an narittokutikaḷ avarkku añcāvāyt tāmum avari etirppavāyiṉa. itaṟkuḷ ūriluḷḷa kuṭimakka ḷellārun tuyiliṉiṉṟu tiṭumeṉa eḻuntu kūkkuraliṭuvā rāyiṉar, itu,
 nariyik kutirip pariyākki
	ñālamellām nikaḻavittup
  periya teṉṉaṉ maturiyellām
	piccatēṟṟum peruntuṟi yāṉ1
eṉṟu aṭikaḷē ōtumāṟṟāl uṇmiyātal teḷiyappaṭum.
narikaḷiṉ ūḷiyoliyum, kāvalar avaṟṟi veruṭṭum oliyum, nakaramāntar tuyilnīṅki eḻuntu accattāliṭuṅ kūkkuraloliyum oruṅkucērntu pēririccali viḷikkavē pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉun tuyili
ṉiṉṟu tiṭukkiṭṭeḻuntu avviriccal nikaḻtaṟku ētu veṉṉiyeṉṟu taṉ meykkāppāḷariyum vāyil kāvalariyum viṉavāniṟkap, parittuṟik kāvalariṟ cilarum virintu vantu avaṉi vaṇaṅki, nēṟṟuvanta putup parikaḷellām iṉṟiraviṉ naṭuyāmattil narikaḷāka māṟip paḻamparikaḷiyuṅ koṉṟu piṭuṅkittiṉṟu kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāy ūḷi yiṭṭuk koṇṭu ūreṅkum ulavā niṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avvaki kaḷiyellām virittuc colli muṟiyiṭṭār.  ac coṟ kēṭṭa maṉṉavaṉ iṭimuḻakkaṅ kēṭṭa araveṉa maṉam maṭintu āṟṟoṇāt tuyaramum, ataṉūṭēyūṭē, ‘parikaḷ narikaḷāṉavāṟu yāṅṅaṉam!  eṉa viyappuṅ koḷvāṉāṉaṉ. civapirāṉ taṉ meyyaṭiyār poruṭṭuc ceyyum aruṭṭiṟaṅkaḷiyum, avaṟṟi avaṉāṟ ceyvittukkoḷḷum meyyaṭiyār uṇmi kaḷiyum aṟiyamāṭṭāta ap pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ, ittakiya māyañ ceytaṟku vātavūraṉ intiracālaṅ kaṟṟavaṉā yiruttal vēṇṭum nām tanta poruṭṭiraḷi yellām vīṇē celaviṭṭu, avaṟṟiṟku īṭukaṭṭuvāṉ pōl narikaḷiyellām parikaḷāka urumāṟṟik koṇar vittu nampāl oppaṭittāṉ! eṉṟu taṉṉuḷḷē eṇṇic cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭāṉ! itaṉālum, ivaṉ veḷḷi nīṟu meyyiṟ kaṇṭu, kaḷḷaṉ kiyiṟ kaṭṭu aviḻppitta varakuṇa pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ ākāṉ eṉpatu piṉṉum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭutal kāṇka.
ivvāṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ cīṟṟamun tuyaramum viyappuṅ koṇṭi rukkiyil pularikkālam vantatu; pakalavaṉ taṉ alarkatirkaḷip parappikkoṇṭu kīḻpāliṟ cempoṟṟiraḷippōṟ ṟōṉṟiṉāṉ. tiruvātavūraṭikaḷ ceykaṭaṉkaḷ kaḻippip, pāṇṭiyaṉik kāṇṭaṟkut tamatu vātavūriṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭuc civikiyūrntu vantār. vantu araṇmaṉi pukuntu pāṇṭiya aracaṉi vaṇaṅki avaṉatu mukatti nōkkiṉavar, atu taḻaliṭippaṭṭa tāmarippōl vāṭiyirukkak kaṇṭu, perumāṉē! vāṭiyiruppa teṉṉi? eṉṟu viṉaviṉār.
ataṟkavaṉ ciṉanaki purintu, nī nalla aṟivuṭiya amiccaṉ! tēṭippārttāl uṉṉiyoppār yāvar! nalla parikaḷ koṇṭuvantaṉi! nī tēṭic ceyta arumi ciṟitō! pollāṅkum vēṟuṇṭō! eṉṟu kaṭukaṭup pōṭu kūṟiṉāṉ. atukēṭṭa aṭikaḷ tuṇukkuṟṟu, vēntar perumāṉē! ciṟantaṉavāka ārāyntu terinteṭuttuk koṇarvitta kutirikaḷuḷ ētēṉum paḻutuṇṭō? nēṟṟu avikaḷi ov vōriṉamāyp pirittuṇarntu aṟiñar muṉṉiliyil akamakiḻntu kikkoṇṭīraṉṟō? eṉṟu viṉaviṉār. accoṟkaḷic cevimaṭutta maṉṉaṉ neruppil ney corintāloppa mēṉmēṟ ciṉaṅkiḷarntu neñcam añcāmal eṉṉi yaṇintu ivaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉiyō? nēṟṟu nī koṇarvitta parikaḷellām narikaḷāki muṉṉamēyuḷḷa emmuṭiya kutirikaḷiyuṅ koṉṟu piṇamākki ūḷiyiṭṭut tirikiṉṟa ceytiyip pittā, nī uṇarntiliyō! āciriyarmāṭṭum aracariṭattum tūya aruḷāḷar pālum aṉpiṟciṟanta tōḻar māṭṭum aruntavat tōriṭattum neñcam añcāmaṟ karavu ceyvōrkkuk koliyallātu vēṟu ceyyat takkatōr oṟuttal uṇṭō! narikaḷiyellām parikaḷākkuvittu aḻittu emmuṭiya kutirippantikaḷiṟ kaṭṭuvittāy! aṟivi mayakki intiracālañ ceyvatu emmiṭattilēyō! vēntarkkuriya poṟṟiraḷi aḻippōr tāmō vīṭupēṟṟiṉiyaṭiyum nallōr! māvēṟik kāṭṭiya kutiric cēvakaṉ eṅkē? avaṉuṭaṉ pōnta cāttavarkaḷ eṅkē! nī maṟinūlkaḷ ōtiṉami yellām iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuttāṉō! niṉṉuṭampiṉi varutti oṟuttalē ceyaṟpālatu, eṉṟu koṭuñ coṟkaḷi vāri iṟittuk, kūṟṟiṉuṅ koṭiyarāṉa taṇṭakkārarkaḷi viḷittu, ivaṉik koṇṭupōyc ciṟiyiliṭṭu vaḷittu, ivaṉ namakkut taravēṇṭum poruḷkaḷiyellām vāṅku
miṉkaḷ! eṉṟu ēviṉāḷ.
avarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē ceytaṟku avari aḻittuc ceṉṟavarkaḷ avaratu pēraṉpiṉ ṟiṟattikkaṇṭu uḷḷam uruki, avaratu uṭampiṉi varuttutaṟku maṉam iciyārāy, avari ōṟāmal viṭiṉum, aracaṉ tamakkut tīṅkupurivāṉeṉa uṉṉi accattāl, avaric ciṟiyil aṭittu vaḷittu vittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ciṟiyil vaḷittuvikkappaṭṭa aṭikaḷ at tuṉpam poṟukka māṭṭāmaṟ pāruruvāya piṟappaṟa vēṇṭum? eṉpataṉi mutalākavuṭiya eṇṇap patikam aruḷicceytu aḻutu kuṟiyirantirukkalāmeṉṟu karutaṟku iṭamirukkiṉṟatu tiruppulampalum innērattil aruḷicceytatā yirukkalā mēṉum, ataṉ mutaṟ ceyyuḷil tiruvārūr kuṟikkap paṭṭiruttalāl, aḵtu attiruk kōyili vaṇaṅkutaṟku aṭikaḷ ceṉṟapōtu aruḷic ceytatā yirukkalāmō eṉṟu aiyuṟutaṟkum iṭañ ceykiṉṟatu. maṟṟoru nērattil avar aruḷicceytatu irumputaru maṉattēṉi eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷi mutalāka vuṭiya tiruvēcaṟavē eṉṟu nampiyār tiruviḷiyāṭal urikku mēl patikam ap patikattiṉkaṇ aṭikaḷ tām tuṉpuṟuvataṉik kūṟāmal, tām tuṉpaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku āṇṭavaṉ nal varuḷāṟ ṟappip piḻittamiyiṉiyum, āṇṭavar tammi añcēl eṉṟu āṇṭa tiṟattiṉiyumē,
nañcāya tuyarkūra naṭuṅkuvēṉ niṉ aruḷāl
  uyñcēṉem perumāṉē yuṭiyāṉē aṭiyēṉi
  añcēleṉṟu āṇṭavā ṟaṉṟē.
eṉṟāṟpōla attiruppatikattil aṭuttaṭuttu ōtutalāṉum, ataṉ oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷil,
aruḷ eṉakku iṅku iṭimarutē
  iṭaṅkoṇṭa ammāṉē
eṉat tiruviṭimarutūrik kiḷantu kūṟutalāṉum ap
patikam in nērattil aruḷicceyyappaṭāmal, aṭikaḷ maturiyiyum amiccurimiyiyum viṭṭakaṉṟu tiruviṭi marutūrkkuc ceṉṟapōtu aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭa tākumeṉpatē tēṟṟamām.
iṉi, aracaṉēvalāl avaṉ taṇṭaṟkārar aṭikaḷik koṇṭupōyc ciṟiyiliṭṭu vaḷittuvittāreṉṟu nampiyār tiruviḷiyāṭal kūṟumēṉum, aṭikaḷiṉ tuyar nīkkumporuṭṭu iṟivaṉ vikiyāṟṟiṟ peruveḷḷattip perukaviṭṭa piṉnikaḻcciyi ārāyntu kāṇumiṭattu aracaṉṟaṉ ēvalar aṭikaḷik koṇṭu pōy vikiyāṟṟu maṇalil naṇpakaṟpōtiṟ kaṭuveyiliṟ niṟutti, avaratu arumit tirumēṉiyi vaḷittu mutukiṉmēl karuṅkalli ēṟṟivittu varuttiṉārātal vēṇṭumeṉpatē karutaṟ pālātā yirukkiṉṟatu. ittakiya pollāk koṭuntuṉpattip poṟāmal aṭikaḷ nintaḻutu pulamputalik kaṇṭa ellāmvalla civapirāṉ mika iraṅkit taṉṟiruvaruḷāṇiyāl ulakiṉik keṭuppatum eṭuppatuñ ceyyum mukiṟkuḻāṅkaḷi niṉintu avi perumaḻi poḻikaveṉat tiruvuḷatteṇṇiṉāṉ. eṇṇutalum aḷavukku miñciya perumaḻi aṉṟikkup poḻintatu.
iṅkoṉṟu ārāyaṟpāṟṟu cittiri vikāci āṉi, āṭi eṉṉum nāṉku tiṅkaḷumē katiravaṉ kaṭuveyil eṟikkum vēṉiṟkālamām. āvaṇit tiṅkaḷō kārkālan tuvaṅkuṅkālamām. aṭikaḷ maturi mānakarkkuk kutiri koṇarvittatu āvaṇittiṅkaḷ mūlanāḷilēyā meṉpatu nampiyār parañcōtiyār eṉṉum iruvar tam tiruviḷi yāṭaṟ purāṇaṅkaṭkun, tiruvātavūrar purāṇattiṟkum oppamuṭintatu. āvaṇi mūlattiṟku aṭutta nāḷilēyē parikaḷellām narikaḷāki maturimānakarip pittēṟac ceytamiyāl, aṉṟikkē pāṇṭiyaṉ aṭikaḷit taṉ ēvalariṭam oppittu varuttiṉāṉātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam avari varuttiya annāḷum at tiṅkaḷum kārkālattu vāṉ talippeyali niṟiyappeytaṟkuriya kālamēyātaliṉ, avvāṟu kārkālattil iyaṟkiyākavē maḻi mikap peytu vikiyāṟu perukiyataṉi iṟivaṉaruḷāl nikaḻntateṉak kūṟutal yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kūṟuvām. māṇikkavācakap perumāṉi amiccarākap peṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ civaperumāṉiṭattum avaṉaṭiyāriṭattum mutirnta pēraṉpu koṇṭavaṉ allaṉ; vēṟṟaracar mēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu avari veṉṟu avartam nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟutalilēyē karuttuṭiyavaṉ. kuṭikaḷiṭattu vāṅkiya iṟipporuḷkaḷi avarkaḷuṭiya naṉmikaḷukkākap payaṉpaṭuttāmal, avaṟṟik koṇṭu uyarnta vāmparikaḷ tiraṭṭit taṉatu kutirip paṭiyi valitākki vēṟṟaracarōṭu pōrpuritaṟku muṉintāṉ. an niṉivuṭiya avaṉ ṟantaporuḷ avaṉōṭu otta niṉivuṭiya ōr amiccar kippaṭāmaṟ, civapirāṉ ṟiruvaṭikkaṭ pēraṉpu pūṇṭu piṟavi vēraṟukkum niṉivuṭiya aṭikaḷ kiyiṟpaṭṭamiyāl, an naṟporuḷ palavakiyāṉa aṟaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu taṉ poruḷākāmal taṉ kuṭikaḷāl tarappaṭṭa  ap poruḷ ak kuṭimakkaḷiṉ naṉmiyiṉ poruṭṭē celavaḻikkap paṭṭataṉi uṇarntum pāṇṭiyaṉ ataṟkuṭaṉpaṭāmal apporuḷukkuk kutiri koḷḷu talilēyē karuttuvittu, ak karutatiṉpaṭi naṭavārāyiṉ aṭikaḷi varuttuvataṟkē uṟuti koṇṭiruntāṉ. ākavē, taṉṉaṭiyavarip pātukāttaṟ poruṭṭum, pāṇṭiyaṉatu pōr vēṭkiyi avittu avaṉatu cerukki aṭakkutaṟporuṭṭumē iṟivaṉ narikaḷip parikaḷākkik koṇarat tiruvuḷam icintāṉ.
iṉip, parikaḷ tirumpa narikaḷāy urumāṟi ōṭip pōkak kaṇṭum ap pāṇṭiyaṉ uḷantirunti aṭikaḷiṉ perumiyuṇarāṉāy avarip peritum varuttuva ṉeṉpatum, aṅṅaṉam varuttumvaḻi maḻiyi mikap poḻivittu viki yāṟṟip perukki veḷḷam karipuraṇṭōṭi nakarkkuṭ pukumāṟu ceyvittāl avvaracaṉ uṇarvu teḷiyap peṟuvaṉ eṉpatum, avvāṟu maḻi poḻivittaṟku ēṟṟatāka ulaka iyaṟkiyil taṉṉāl vakukkappaṭṭa kālam āvaṇit tiṅkaḷē yātaliṉ in nikaḻccikaṭku icintuvarumāṟu kutirikaḷi at tiṅkaḷiṉ mūlanāḷilēyē koṇṭu cellal vēṇṭumeṉpatum ellāmvalla iṟivaṉ muṉṉarē taṉ ṟiruvuḷattaṭittuk kārkālattiṉ kaṇṇēyē aṅṅaṉam maḻiyi nirampap peyvittut tāṉ karutiyati muṭittāṉeṉka. aṟṟēl, narikaḷ parikaḷākavum, apparikaḷ tirumpa narikaḷākavum ulaka
viyaṟki nikaḻccikku māṟāyc ceytu kāṭṭiyatupōlak, kār maḻi peytaṟku ēṟṟatallāta vēṉiṟkālattil atu poḻiyumāṟu ceytukāṭṭātavā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ; mutaṉ muṟi iyaṟki nikaḻccikku māṟāyc ceytukāṭṭiya naripari māṟṟattiṉ arumiyiyē uyttuṇaramāṭṭātu ataṉi intiracālameṉa ikaḻntu aṭikaḷi varuttiya ap pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉukku mēlum aṅṅaṉam oṉṟu ceytu kāṭṭutalāṟ pōtarum payaṉ oṉṟiṉmiyāṉum, taṉṉaṭiyārik kāttalē iṟivaṉ ṟiruvuḷak kuṟippākalāṉum, iraṇṭāmuṟi avarik kāttal vēṇṭi iyaṟki nikaḻcciyōṭu oppavē vittu maḻiyip poḻivittāṉeṉka. oṉṟi iyaṟki nikaḻcci yōṭu ovvāmal vittuk kāṭṭiyatu taṉṉaṭiyavariṉ perumi tōṟṟuvittu avarip pātukāttaṟkum, maṟṟoṉṟi an nikaḻcciyōṭu oppavittuk kāṭṭiyatu muṉṉiyatāl uṇarvu teḷiyap peṟāta ammaṉṉaṉi oṟuttut taṉṉaṭiyavarip pātukāttaṟkumēyā meṉka.
allatūum, kīḻkaṭaṟkari maruṅkēyuḷḷa ūrkaḷiṟ kārkālattil maḻi tappātu peytal pōlak, kaṭaṟ kariyi mika akaṉṟirukkum uḷnāṭukaḷil akkālattilum maḻi tappātu peytal illiyākum; pāṇṭināṭu kīḻ kaṭaṟkarikku arukilillāmal eṭṭi uḷḷiṭattil iruppatoṉṟākiyāl, ataṉkaṭ kār kālattil maḻipeyyātu pōtalum ataṉāl an nāṭṭil aṭut taṭuttu vaṟkaṭam uṇṭātalum paḻiya nūlkaḷālum ulakattār uriyāṉum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉavēyām. in nikaḻcci yuṇṭāṉa ap paḻiyakālattiluṅ kūṭa maturimā nakarattilum ataṉic cūḻnta iṭaṅkaḷilum vēṉiṟkāla veppan taṇiyāmal aḵtu āvaṇit tiṅkaḷ muṟṟun toṭarn tiruntamiyālaṉṟō pāṇṭiyaṉ ēvalāṭkaḷ aṭikaḷi akkaṭuveyiliṟ koṇṭupōy niṟuttivittu naliva rāyiṉar. aṅṅaṉam maḻipeytal aritāya avvāvaṇit tiṅkaḷil aṭikaḷi avar varuttiya aṉṟikku aḷavu paṭāp perumaḻi poḻintu vikiyāṟum uṭip peṭuttatāyiṉ, aḵtu aṭikaḷik kāttaṟporuṭṭākavē iṟivaṉ āṇiyāl nikaḻnta putumiyāmeṉṟē koḷḷaṟ pāṟṟu.
avvāṟu koḷḷām, iyaṟkiyāy nikaḻnta an nikaḻcciyē aṭikaḷik kāttaṟkum utaviyāyiṉatu kākki ēṟap paṉampaḻam vīḻnta nikaḻcciyō ṭoppatē  yāmeṉiṉ; aṭikaḷiṉ poṟuttaṟkariya tuṉpattip pōkkutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātatāṉa an nērattil am maḻi poḻiyumāṟu toṭarpu paṭuttiyatu kaṭavuṭ ceyalē yallāmaṟ piṟiteṉṉi? oru pēraraṟiviṉ ceyalāl untappaṭāmal aṟivillā maḻi tāṉākavē annērattiṟ peytateṉṟu urikkum aṟivillā vaḻakkup payaṉuṭiyatō, taṉṉaṭi yārik kākkum poruṭṭu muṟṟaṟivuṭiya oru muḻumutaṟ poruḷāl ēvappaṭṭu aḵtu an nērattiṟ peytateṉ ṟurikkum aṟivuṭi vaḻakkup payaṉuṭiyatō eṉṟu ārāyntu pārkkavalla nuṇṇaṟivuṭiyārkku, ellā aṟivumuṭiya iṟivaṉaruḷāṟ celuttappaṭṭu aḵtu aṅṅaṉam peytateṉak koḷḷutalē vāyppuṭit tāyt tōṉṟum. ellā aṟivum āṟṟalum uṭiya oru kaṭavuḷiṉ ṟiruvaruḷāl ulakamum ulakattu nikaḻccikaḷum naṭi peṟukiṉṟaṉa veṉak kūṟutal tamatu aṟivukkup perumiyākā teṉṟu karuti, ellām aṟiviṉṟi iyaṟkiyākavē iyaṅku kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu aṟiyāmikkē ēṟṟañcolli makiḻvōr tammi oruvar aṟivillātavar eṉavum, tām ceyvaṉa vellām aṟiviṉmiyēyām eṉavuṅ kūṟiyakkāl ac coṟkēṭṭu varuntutal eṉṉiyō? ataṉāl, aṟiyāmikku ēṟṟañ collum avaratu uḷḷamē avartam koḷkikku māṟāy niṟṟaliṉ, ulakattu nikaḻccikaḷ ellām elliyaṟṟa oru pēraṟivāl toṭarpu paṭuttappaṭṭu nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatē tēṟṟamām. ākavē, aṭikaḷ poruṭṭu narikaḷip parikaḷākki muṉṉināṭ koṇarnta iṟivaṉē avarkkup piṉṉi nāḷ nērnta koṭun tuṉpattiṉiyun tuṭittaṟ poruṭṭu aṅṅaṉam perumaḻi peyvittu aruḷiṉā ṉeṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka.
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	āṉantamāli, (7)


7. iṟivaṉ 
maṇcumantu aṭipaṭutal
ivvāṟu aḷavukku miñcip peyta perumaḻiyiṉāl veḷḷam peruki vikiyāṟṟil puraṇṭu varalāyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷi avyāṟṟu maṇalil niṟutti varuttiya taṇṭaṟkārarum avari viṭuttut tattam maṉiyakam nōkki ōṭiṉar. tammik kāttaruḷiya civapirāṉ tiruvaruḷi niṉintu niṉinturukiya uḷḷattiṉarāyum aḻutakaṇṇiṉa rāyum aṭikaḷum vikiyāṟṟi viṭṭu ataṉ karimī tēṟic cokkap perumāṉ tirukkōyiluṭ ceṉṟu avaṉit toḻutu vaḻuttiyapaṭiyāy iruntār.
itaṟkuḷ avyāṟṟiṉ veḷḷaṅ karikaḷi neṭuka uṭittu maturimā nakarkkuḷ eṅkum pukuntu nirampi mēṉmēl uyartalkaṇṭa an nakarattu māntarellāṅ kūkkuraliṭṭu ōlamiṭuvārāṉār. ivikaḷi yuṇarnta pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ neñcaṅ kalaṅkit tiruvātavūraṭikaḷi yāṉ oṟuttup perumpiḻi ceytamiyāl ippēriṭar nērntatu pōlum! eṉ ceyvōm! eṉa uḷamvaruntit, taṉ amiccariyellāṅ kūuy eṉṟum vārā ipperu veḷḷam iṉṟuvantu in nakari aḻippāṉpukka teṉṉi! itaṟkuṭaṉē ceyaṟpālatu yātu? eṉṟu viṉavutalum, avarellām muṉnāḷ piṉnāḷ nikaḻccikaḷi naṉkuṇarntu pārttut, tiruvātavūrar civapirāṉmāṭṭu varampikanta pēraṉpuṭiyarāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, avaratu taṉmiyuṇarātē avari varuttip piḻiceytataṉālē tāṉ itu nērntatu! avari varuvittu vaṇaṅki avaratu aruḷip peṟṟāl maṭṭum ivveḷḷan taṇiyum, eṉa viṭi pakarntārkaḷ. taṉatu karuttum avarkaḷatu karuttum ottirukkavē pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉ ceytavi yellām piḻiyeṉat teḷintu, cokkapperumāṉ tirukkōyili lirunta aṭikaḷi varuvittut tāṉ ceypiḻiyiṉip poṟukkumpaṭi vēṇṭi, veḷḷam ūrkkuḷḷē pukāmal vaṭintupōkumpaṭi paṇittaruḷal vēṇṭum, eṉak kuṟiyirantu, uṭipaṭṭa karikaḷiyum ūriluḷḷār aṉivarum oruṅkuvantu niṉṟu aṭikkumāṟum tēvarīrē talivarāy niṉṟu ceytal vēṇṭu meṉavum paṇivuṭaṉ moḻintāṉ.
muṉṉar aracaṉ tammēṟ ciṉantakālattum piṉṉar ippōtu makiḻnta kālattum veṟuppu viruppaṟṟu oru niliyil niṉṟa uḷḷattiṉarāṉa aṭikaḷ tammiyāṭ koṇṭa aiyaṉaruḷi viyantu veḷippōntu, parimēl vanta perumāṉatu tiruvuruvatti uḷḷattil uruki uṉṉi veḷḷan taṇintu ēkumāṟu vēṇṭiyavāṟāy viki yāṟṟaṅ kariyil, vēntaṉ paṇi ceyvāroṭum vantu cērntār. cērntu, maturimā nakaril uḷḷa ovvoru vīṭṭārum uṭintupōṉa kariyil ivvivvaḷavu iṭam aṭittuk kariyi uyarttutal vēṇṭumeṉa ap paṇiyāḷārāṟ paṟiyaṟivittu, aṟṟināḷ mālikkuḷ avarellām avi yaṭipaṭumāṟu ceyvikka veṉavuṅ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭaruḷiṉār. taṉ aṭiyār tuyarkaḷiyum poruṭṭu veḷḷattip peruka viṭṭa iṟivaṉ tuyaroḻinta aṭikaḷiṉ vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅki aḵtu ūriviṭṭu vaṭintēkavuñ ceytāṉ. paṟi yaṟivippuk kēṭṭa ūravarellām virintuvantu tattamakku aḷantuviṭṭa kariyiṉi aṭikkum muyaṟciyil muṉintu niṟka, appaṇiyāḷar avari attoḻilil virintēvutaṟkaṇ muṟuki niṟpārāṉār.
iḵtiṅṅaṉam naṭavāniṟka, am maturimā nakariṟ
ṟeṉkiḻakku mūliyiluḷḷa orumaṉiyil piṭṭuccuṭṭu viṟṟup piḻikkum nari mutiyōḷāṉa oru piṭṭu vāṇicci iruntaṉaḷ. avaḷ civapirāṉmāṭṭup peruki mutirnta pēraṉpuṭiyavaḷ. avaḷukku neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarāyiruntā rellām iṟantupaṭṭamiyiṉ, avaḷ taṉṉan taṉiyāḷā yiruntu piṭṭu viṟpaṉiyil varum mikac ciṟu ūtiyap poruḷikkoṇṭu uyir vāḻntu varum ēḻiyāvaḷ. ēṉiyōrkku aḷantuviṭṭa paṅkukaḷi yellān taṅ kūliyāṭkaḷik koṇṭu aṭippippōrum, kūliyāṭkaḷ kiṭikkap peṟāviṭiṉ tāmē yaṭippārumāy niṟka, ip piṭṭu vāṇiccikku aḷantuviṭṭa paṅkō mutuku kūṉi valiviḻanta avaḷ taṉṉāl aṭikkak kūṭāmalum takka kūlikoṭukkap poruḷillātavaḷākaliṉ kūliyāḷ koṇṭu atuceyya iyalāmalum vaṟitē kiṭantatu.
ataṉik kaṇṭa vēntaṉ paṇiyāḷar avaḷpāṟ ceṉṟu avaḷi nerukka, avaḷ attuṉpam poṟukka māṭṭāmal taṉatu nilimikkēṟṟa kūliyāḷ koḷḷum poruṭṭu annakareṅkum mellamella uḻaṉṟu tirintum evaruṅ kiṭikkap peṟāmiyāl, neñcam añcik kalaṅki, mēluntirivataṟku iyalāmal iḷippuṟṟuc cokkappirāṉ tirukkōyil vāyiliṟ ceṉṟu niṉṟu, aḻutu kuṟiyirap pāḷāṉāḷ. ivvāṟavaḷ niṟkiyil, taliyiṟ kaviḻtta kūṭiyum tōḷmēl māṭṭiya maṇ veṭṭiyuṅ koṇṭu, puḻutipaṭinta mēṉiyaṉāy, ariyil aḻukkaṭinta paḻantuṇiyuṭuttavaṉāyk kaṭṭaḻakiṟ ciṟanta ōr iḷiya kūliyāḷ ‘eṉṉik kūlikku ēval koḷvār uṇṭō? eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭu avvaḻiyē varakkaṇṭāḷ.
kaṇṭatun, tāṉ uṟṟa tuyarañ ciṟitu taṇiyap peṟṟāḷāy ak kūliyāḷi arukaḻittu, mintaṉē! vikiyāṟṟaṅkariyuṭippil eṉakku aḷantuviṭ
ṭirukkum paṅki aṭittut taruvāyō? eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. ataṟkavaṉ, aṉṉāy! appaṭiyē aṭittut tarukiṉṟēṉ. eṉakku yātu kūlikoṭuppāy? eṉṟāṉ. appaṉē! uṉakkuk kūlikoṭukka eṉkiyiṟ poruḷilli. yāṉ cuṭṭuviṟkum piṭṭiṉiyē uṉakkuk kūliyākat taruvēṉ. atu tiṉpataṟkuc cuṭaccuṭa naṟuvitāy maṇamuṭiyatāyirukkum, eṉṟaṉaḷ ammutiyōḷ. ammē! nī vaṭṭamāyc cuṭṭu viṟkum muḻuppiṭṭukkūṭak koṭukka vēṇṭuvatilli. appiṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu utirvaṉavaṟṟi eṉakkuk kūliyākak koṭuttāl aviyē pōtum. ippōteṉakku mikap pacikkiṉṟatu. utirntupōṉa cuvippiṭṭi eṉakku iṭṭāl ataṉi muntat tiṉṟu iḷippāṟip pinti niṉ kariyip pōyk kaṭṭuvēṉ, eṉṟāṉ akkūliyāḷ. ataṟkavaḷ makiḻvuṭaṉicintu aṅṅaṉamē tanta piṭṭiṉutirvi avaṉ ēṟṟut taṉatu arittuṇiyiṟ kaṭṭivittu, ‘itaṉi yoppatoru cuvippaṇṭam vēṟilli! eṉac colliya vāṟāy ataṉit tiṉṟukoṇṭē vikiyāṟṟaṅkari maruṅku ceṉṟāṉ.
am mutiyōḷum taṉakkuriya kōlaṟiyik kāṭṭum poruṭṭu avaṉōṭu kūṭavē vikiyāṟṟaṅ karikkut tāṉuñ ceṉṟāḷ. ceṉṟu, aṅkuniṉṟa aracaṉ kaṇakkar avaṉip piṭṭu vāṇicciyiṉ kūliyāḷ eṉṟu tamatu ēṭṭil varintu koḷḷumāṟu ceyvittu, maṉamakiḻvōṭun taṉatu maṉiyakan tirumpip piṭṭuc cuṭṭiruppāḷāṉāḷ.
ivaḷatu paṅkākiya kōlaṟiyi aṭikkac ceṉṟa akkūliyāḷō ataṉi aṭikkun toḻilic ceyyāmal, am mutiyōḷpāṟ peṟṟuvanta ap piṭṭi iṭiyiṭiyē taṉatu maṭiyiṉiṉṟum eṭutteṭuttut tiṉpatum, itu mikka cuviyiṉiyuṭittu eṉṟu collik koṇṭē āṭuvatum pāṭuvatuñ ceyyā niṉṟāṉ. aracaṉṟaṉ ēvalāḷar taṉṉarukē varakkaṇṭāl orukūṭi maṇṇi veṭṭi eṭuttuc cumantu koṇṭu mellappōyk kariyiṟ koṭṭi ataṉāl iḷippuṟṟāṉpōl niṟpaṉ. ivvāṟu maṇ cumanta uḻippāl viyarvi mika varappeṟṟu ataṉik kaḻuvu vāṉpōl avvāṟṟu nīruṭceṉṟu kuḷippatum, ataṉ kaṇ nīnti viḷiyāṭuvatuñ ceyvaṉ. piṉṉark kariyēṟi ēṉik kūliyāṭkaḷiyellām nōkkip pēcuvatum nakippatuñ ceyvaṉ. ataṉpiṟ ṟaṉakkup paciyeḻuntateṉak kūṟik koṇṭu am mutiyōḷpāl iṭiyiṭiyē ceṉṟu ammē! maṇcumanta uḻippāl eṉakku mettap pacikkiṉṟatu, utirnta piṭṭi yiṭu’ eṉṟu kēṭṭu, avaḷum cuṭaccuṭap perumpālum utirntu pōṉa naṟuñcuvip piṭṭi yiṭa, avaṟṟip peṟṟut tiṉṟukoṇṭē av yāṟṟaṅkarikku mīḷvaṉ, mīṇṭu, muṉpōlavē ciṟitu maṇṇic cumantum, ataṉik kariyiliṭṭum, piṉṉar nīril nīnti viḷiyāṭiyum, kariyēṟiyāṭiyum, maṟṟik kūliyāṭ kaḷōṭu coṟpōr toṭuttum, piṉṉar avarōṭu nakittum, maṉṉavar kāvalar kaṭukiṉ ataṟkañcuvāṉpōṟ kāṭṭi avari ēmāṟṟiyum, avarkaḷ taṉṉip piṭittaṭikka neruṅkiṉāl virintōṭi āḻamāṉa āṟṟuveḷḷatti ṉiṭi nīntic ceṉṟum, kaṇṭārellām ‘ivaṉ pittaṉō! eṉṟu colla vēli ceyyāmal ataṉi maḻuppiviṭalāṉāṉ. uccippakal kaḻintu, ñāyiṟu mēlpāṟ cāyum piṟpakal varutalum, vēli ceytamiyāl vanta ayarvu tīrppāṉ pōl, maṟṟavarkkut teriyāmal maṟivāyc ceṉṟu av vāṟṟaṅkari maruṅkiṉatoru koṉṟimara nīḻalil maṇali aṇiyākac cērttu, maṇ kūṭiyit taliyaṇiyāka vittukkoṇṭu tuyilvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
ivaṉ ivvāṟu tuyililirukka mālippoḻutum vantatu. ūrār ellām tattamakku aḷantuviṭṭa kōlaṟikaḷi aṭittu, yāṟṟaṅkariyiyum uyarttu viṭṭaṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ karimuḻutum aṭikkap paṭṭatāveṉak kāṇum poruṭṭut, tiruvātavūraṭi
kaḷit talimiyākkoṇṭa amiccar kuḻāt
tōṭum vikiyāṟṟaṅ kariyarukē vantucērntāṉ. cērntatum, aṅkuḷḷa kari yuṭippukaḷi yellām variciyākap pārttukkoṇṭu vantu kaṭippaṭiyākap piṭṭuvāṇicci paṅkaṇṭi varutalum, aḵtu aṭi
paṭāmal irukkavum, ataṉ vaḻiyē veḷḷam mikuntu perukip pakkattuḷḷa karikaḷiyum aṟukkavuṅ kaṇṭu pāṇṭiyaṉ katumeṉac ciṉaṅkoṇṭu, maruṅkē niṉṟa ēvalari nōkki iḵtēṉ aṭikkappaṭavilli? eṉṟu uṟukki viṉāva, avarkaḷuḷ talivaṉāvāṉ aracaṉip paṇintu vēntar perumāṉē! itu mutiyōḷāṉa piṭṭuvāṇicci kōlaṟi; avaḷukkuk kūliyāḷarāy vanta koṟṟāḷ orukūṭi maṇṇāvatu veṭṭiyiṭṭāṉilli; avaṉ iṉṟu muḻutum viḷiyāṭṭilēyē kālaṅkaḻittāṉ; avaṉip piṭittu aṭittu vēlivāṅka evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum avaṉ em kiyiṟ piṭipaṭṭāṉilli, eṉṟu añcik kūṟiṉāṉ. ataṉikkēṭṭa aracaṉ piṉṉum peruñ cīṟṟamuṟṟu ā! appaṭiyā! avaṉi eppaṭiyāvatu tēṭippiṭittu eṉpāṟ koṇarmiṉkaḷ! eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
uṭaṉē aracaṉ kāvalar palar aṅkumiṅkumāy ōṭittēṭa, akkoṟṟāḷ akkariyiṉ ōr otuk
kiṭattē oru koṉṟimara nīḻaliṟ paṭuttu uṟaṅkak kaṇṭu avaṉip piṭittaṟku neruṅkiṉārkaḷ. avar neruṅkutalik kaṇṭa akkoṟṟāḷ tiṭumeṉa eḻuntu, taṉ maṇ veṭṭiyāl maṇṇiyeṭuttuk kūṭiyiliṭṭu ataṉic cumantukoṇṭu vēli ceyvāṉ pōla mella iyaṅka, avvēvalar avaṉip pōyp paṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. paṟṟiyavāṟē avaṉi īrttuk koṇarntu pāṇṭiyaṉetirē niṟutta, avaṉ akkoṟṟāḷi nōkki, ēṭā! piṭṭu vāṇiccikku aḷantuviṭṭa ikkōlaṟiyi aṭiyāmal nī iṉṟu muḻutum viḷiyāṭiyateṉṉi? eṉṟu ciṉantu
viṉāva, avaṉ ētu muriyāmal vāḷāniṉṟaṉaṉ. atukaṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ piṉṉuñ cīṟṟam mikkut, taṉkiyiṟ poṟpiram poṉṟi vāṅki ōṅki vīci akkoṟṟāḷiṉ mutukiṉmēṟ puṭittaṉaṉ.
puṭittatum avvaṭi cuṟīreṉap pāṇṭiyaṉ mutukilum, tiruvātavūraṭikaḷi yuḷḷiṭṭa amiccar mutukilum, aracaṉēvalar mutukilum, ūravar ellār mutukilum uyiruṭip poruḷkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ mutukilum paṭṭatu; atē nērattil ellār vāyil niṉṟum ‘ō! eṉṉum ōr alaṟutaloli eḻuntatu; iṅṅaṉam akkoṟṟāḷi aṭitta aṭi ellār mutukilumpaṭṭa ōr imippoḻutiṟkuḷ avaṉ taṉtaliyiṟ cumantirunta oru kūṭi maṇṇiyum uṭipaṭṭirunta piṭṭu vāṇicci paṅkiṟ koṭṭiṉāṉ; avvuṭippum aṭipaṭṭatu; āṉāl tam kaṇ etirē ceṉṟa akkoṟṟāḷi evaruṅ kaṇṭilar.
uṭaṉē, aṅku niṉṟa tiruvātavūraṭikaḷ, aṭipaṭṭu maṟinta akkoṟṟāḷ civapirāṉēyātal teḷintu; aṭiyaṟṟa paṉipōl nilattiṉmēl viḻuntu puraṇṭu kaṭavuḷarkkum ariyiyākiya nī nāyiṉēmporuṭṭu eḷiya koṟḷāḷāp pōntaṉiyō! pōntu tirumuṭimēl maṇṇuñ cumantu tirumēṉiyilē aṭiyum paṭṭaṉiyō! eṉap palavāṟu kūṟit tēmpit tēmpiyaḻutu pulampu vārāṉār. avaratu āṟṟāmiyik kaṇṭum, koṟṟāḷi yaṭitta aṭi ellār mutukilum paṭṭamiyum, ōr imip poḻutiṟkuḷ oru kūṭi maṇṇāl appēruṭippiṉi aṭittu avaṉ maṟintamiyum nērē pārttum pāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉōṭu aṅkuniṉṟā rellārum karikaṭanta viyappum āṟṟāmiyu muṭiyarāy uḷḷaṅ kariyak kaṇṇīr yāṟāy oḻuka aḻutu varuntuvārāṉār. ippeṟṟittāka iṟivaṉip pirintu āṟṟā nērattē, neñci nīrāy urukkum cettilāppattup pōṉṟa cila patikaṅkaḷi aṭikaḷ aruḷicceytirukkalāmeṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. civaperumāṉ, taṉpāṟ pēraṉpiṉaḷākiya oru piṭṭuvāṇicci, poruṭṭākavē oru koṟṟāḷāyvantu, piṭṭamutuceytu, taṉ tirumuṭi
mēl maṇṇuñ cumanta aruṭṭiṟatti nērē kaṇ
ṭiruntamiyālaṉṟō aṭikaḷ.
āṅkatu taṉṉil aṭiyavaṭ kākap
  pāṅkāy maṇcuman taruḷiya paricum1
eṉṟum,
 tiṇpōr viṭiyāṉ civapurattār pōrēṟu
  maṇpāṉ maturiyiṟ piṭṭamutu ceytaruḷit
  taṇṭālē pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉṉip paṇikoṇṭa
  puṇpāṭal pāṭinām pūvalli koyyāmō2
eṉṟum, 
piṭṭu nērpaṭa maṇcumanta
  peruntuṟip perumpittaṉē.3
eṉṟum tamatu arumit tiruvācakattiṟ palaviṭattum eṭuttōtuvārāyiṉar.
koṟṟāḷāy vanta apperumāṉ pittaṉip
pōl āṭip pāṭi viḷiyāṭi vēli ceyyātu tirintami yiṉik kaṇṭa ēṉiyōr avaṉip ‘pittaṉō! eṉṟu kūṟiṉami terittaṟkē, piṭṭu nērpaṭa maṇcumanta
	peruntuṟip perumpittaṉē
eṉṟaruḷic ceytār.
iṉi, akkoṟṟāḷ nāḷmuḻutuṅ kariyaṭiyāmaṟ kālam pōkkiṉami kaṇṭu ciṉantu aṟṟi māliyil avaṉi aṭittavar aracaṉ ṟaṉ ēvalarē eṉa nampiyār tiruviḷiyāṭalum tiruvātavūrar purāṇamuṅ kūṟāniṟkap parañcōtiyār tiruviḷiyāṭal oṉṟumē appōtu akkoṟṟāḷi aṭittavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉak kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu; maṟṟut tiruvātavūraṭikaḷō,
 kaṇcumanta neṟṟik kaṭavuḷ kalimaturi
  maṇcumantu kūlikoṇṭu akkōvāṉ mottuṇṭu
  puṇcumanta poṉmēṉi pāṭutuṅkāṇ ammāṉāy4
eṉavum,
 maṇpāṉ maturiyiṟ piṭṭamutu ceytaruḷit
  taṇṭālē pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉṉip paṇikoṇṭa
  puṇpāṭal pāṭinām pūvalli koyyāmō5
eṉavum, nēriruntukaṇṭu pāṇṭiyaṉē civapirāṉit taṇṭālē aṭittaṉaṉeṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, ēṉi yiraṇṭu purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ kūṟṟum itaṉkaṭ piḻipaṭukiṉṟa teṉavum, parañcōtiyār tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ kūṟṟoṉṟumē īṇṭup poruttamuṭittāy niṟkiṉṟa teṉavun tērntuṇarntu koḷka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	kīrttit tiruvakaval, (46, 47)
2.	tiruppūvalli, (16)
3. 	tirukkaḻukkuṉṟam patikam, (2)
4.	tiruvammāṉi, (8) 
5.	tiruppūvalli, (16)


8. pāṇṭiyaṉ
piḻipoṟukka vēṇṭutal
iṉic, civaperumāṉ kaṭavuḷarkkuṅ kāṭṭāta taṉatu arumit tiruvuruvit tamporuṭṭuk kāṭṭit tirumēṉimici aṭiyum uṇṭa pēraruṭṭiṟatti niṉintu niṉintu aṭikaḷ peritum āṟṟārāy akkarimēṟ puraṇṭu puraṇṭu alaṟutalik kaṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ neñcam nekkuruki, uḷḷam poṟāṉāy avartiru muṉṉarppōntu kīḻviḻuntu vaṇaṅki, vātavūr vanta vaḷḷaṟ perumāṉē! tēvarīr tamiyaṉēṟku amiccarāy vantaruḷiyatu ellāmvalla civapirāṉ parimītu eḻuntaruḷi vantu oṉṟukkum paṟṟāta puḻuttali nāyiṉēṉukkun taṉatu arumit tirukkōlattik kāṭṭi eṉṉip piṇitta aṟiyāmik kaṭṭi aṟuttaṟkēyaṉṟō! maṟittavak koḻuntē! aṉpiṉ kaṉiyē! eṉatu karu māḷak kāṭṭiya at tirukkōlattiṉ arumiyuṇarātē pēyaṉēṉ pulliya āṭiyoṉṟum emperumāṉukku aḷittup piḻi ceytēṉē! em perumāṉi ēvalkoṇṭa aṭikaḷiṉ meyttavap pēraṉpiṉ peṟṟiyi aṟiyāmē, tīviṉik kētuvākiya celvap poruḷi vēṇṭit tēvarīr tirumēṉiyiyum varuntac ceyta koṭiyaṉēṉ ciṟumiyāṟ ceyta piḻikaḷiyellām poṟuttaruḷvīrō! aṉpiṉ viḷakkē! āṇṭavaṉē! muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya aiyaṉ tūmuṭimēl maṇṇuñ cumantu pollāp pāviyēṉatu koṭuviṉik kiyāl aṭiyumpaṭṭatu tēvarīr poruṭṭu eṉṉum pukaḻ oṉṟumē tēvarīrkkup pōtumē! in nilatti āḷum aracu tēvarīratēyāka aṭiyēṉ tēvarīrukku nāḷum paṇiceytoḻukumāṟu kaṭikkaṇittaruḷal vēṇṭum! eṉṟu kaṇṇīrvāra aḻutu kuṟiyirantāṉ.
ac coṟkaḷic cevimaṭutta aṭikaḷ tām amic curimiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkit tirupperuntuṟi cellutaṟku ituvē vāyppāṉa nērameṉat tērntu eḻuntu, maṉṉar pirāṉē! numakku yāṉ amiccaṉāyirukkap peṟṟamiyā laṉṟō nāykkut tavicu iṭṭāleṉṉa yāṉ iṅṅaṉa mellām eṉ āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruṭpēṟṟiṟku uriyaṉākap peṟṟēṉ. nīr eṉṉi ivvamiccurimiyiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittu, yāṉ tirupperuntuṟikku ēkumāṟu viṭi koṭuppa toṉṟē eṉakku aracavāḻvu aḷippatākum, eṉṟār. atukēṭṭa maṉṉavaṉ avarip piritaṟku āṟṟāṉāy mika varunti, ellā vulakaṅkaḷukkum uyir kaḷukkum mutalaraca rākiya civaperumāṉ ṟiruvaṭikaḷic cārnta meyyaṭiyarākiya avar iṉit taṉpāl irār eṉpatit teḷiyavuṇarntu, tēvarīr tiruvuḷap pāṅkiṉpaṭiyē ceytaruḷuka! eṉṟu kūṟi, avar tiruvaṭikaḷil vīḻntu iṟiñcit, taṉ araṇmaṉikku ēkiṉāṉ.
ataṉ piṉ uṭaṉē, aṭikaḷ tām aṇintirunta amiccark kuriya kōlattik kaḷittu, civaperumāṉ tiruvaṭit toṇṭupēṇum naṟṟavakkōlam pūṇṭu, ālavāy aṇṇaluḻicceṉṟu aṉpāl akaṅ karinturuki yētti viṭikoṇṭu, tam perumāṉ tammik kuruvaṭiviṟ pōntu āṇṭaruḷiya tirupperuntuṟikku virintēkiṉār.
ivvaḷavil, nampiyār tiruviḷiyāṭal aṭikaḷ varalāṟṟik kūṟi mēl naṭappaṉavaṟṟi iraṇṭu ceyyuṭ kaḷiṟ pāṭi muṭittuviṭṭatu. parañcōti muṉivarō, maturiyi viṭṭakaṉṟapiṉ aṭikaḷ civapirāṉ tiruk kōyilkaḷ palavum iṟiñcik kaṭippaṭiyākat tilli yampalañceṉṟu puttari vaḻakkil veṉṟu civapirāṉ ṟiruvaṭinīḻal eytiṉāreṉap piṉvaralāṟṟic curukki muṭittuviṭṭār. maṟṟuk kaṭavuṇmāmuṉivar iyaṟṟiya tiruvātavūrar purāṇam maṭṭumē aṭikaḷiṉ piṉvaralāṟṟi muṟṟaveṭuttuk kūṟutaliṉ, ataṉvaḻiyē ceṉṟu ataṉkaṭ poruntuvaṉavaṟṟi yeṭuttu oru kōvippaṭuttu aṭikaḷiṉ piṉvaralāṟṟiṉi varikuvām;
mēṟcolliyavāṟu aṭikaḷ maturimānakari viṭṭakaṉṟu tirupperuntuṟikku mīṇṭapōtu, muṉṉē avarik kuruvaṭivil vantu āṭkoṇṭa perumāṉun tirut toṇṭarkaḷum piṉṉum aṅkēyē eḻuntaruḷiyirun taṉareṉat tiruvātavūrar purāṇaṅ kūṟum. muṉṉē kuruvaṭivil eḻuntaruḷiya mutalvaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷiyāṭkoṇṭu avarkkuc ceviyaṟivuṟut taruḷiyatu ainteḻuttālāya oru colliṉ muṭinta vuṇmiyēyāmeṉpatu.
maṉṉa eṉṉiyōr vārttiyuṭpaṭuttup, paṟṟiṉāy1
eṉṟu aṭikaḷē ōtumāṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭutalāṉum, avvāṟāṭ koṇṭaruḷiyavuṭaṉē akkuravaṉ taṉṉaṭiyār kuḻāt toṭum maṟintaruḷiṉami,
toṇṭa ṉēṟkuuḷḷa vāvantu tōṉṟiṉāy
  kaṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilēṉ eṉṉa kaṇ māyamē2
eṉṟu aṭikaḷē aruḷicceyyumāṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭutalāṉum, aṅṅaṉaṅ kuravaṉ maṟintavaḻi aṭikaḷit taṉiyē viṭuttup pōyiṉāṉeṉpatu,
aratta mēṉiyāy aruḷcey aṉparum nīyum
aṅkeḻun taruḷi iṅkueṉi, iruttiṉāy muṟiyō3
eṉpataṉāṟ ṟeḷiyak kiṭattalāṉum, kuruntamara nīḻalil muṉṉarorukāl tam kuravaṉik kaṇṭa aṭikaḷ piṉṉar ak kuruvaṭivil avaṉi orunāḷuṅ kaṇṭilareṉpatu,
kāṇumāṟu kāṇēṉ uṉṉi annāṭ kaṇṭēṉum4
eṉṉum aṭikaḷ tirumoḻiyāṉē naṉkuṇarak kiṭattalāṉum tiruvātavūrār purāṇak kūṟṟu uṇmi nikaḻcciyoṭu māṟupaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ, aḵtu ēṟṟukkoḷḷaṟ pāṟṟaṉṟu.
aḵtu aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, aṭikaḷ mīṇṭun tirup peruntuṟikkuc ceṉṟapiṉ taṅkuravaṉ eḻuntaruḷit tammi āṭkoṇṭa kuruntamarattip piriyārāy, ataṉarukiruntu, taṅkuravaṉi niṉintu niṉintu āṟṟāmimikku aḻutaḻutu tiruccatakam pāṭiyapaṭiyāyt, taṅ kuravaṉatu arumit tiruvuruviṟ ṟamatu niṉivip patittut tammit taṉpāl iṟivaṉ varuvittuk koḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭiyiruppa, orunāḷ iṟivaṉ avarkkuk kaṉaviṟ ṟōṉṟi, āṇṭuḷḷa moyyār taṭampoykiyik kāṭṭi itaṉkaṇ orunāḷ taḻalvaṭiviṟṟōṉṟi niṉakkum niṉṉic cūḻnta aṭiyārkkuṅ kāṭci tantaruḷuvēm; appōtu niṉṉic cūḻnta aṭiyār palarum at taḻaliṉkaṭ ceṉṟu vīḻntu maṟikuvar; āṉāl, nī maṭṭum avvāṟu ataṉkaṭ ceṉṟu vīḻaṟka! ataṉik kaṇṭuiṟiñciya piṉṉart tiruvuttarakōcamaṅki ceṉṟu āṇṭum aṅṅaṉamē tōṉṟum taḻal vaṭivik kaṇṭu toḻutu, ataṉpiṉ yām eḻuntaruḷiyirukkun tiruk
kōyilkaṭōṟuñ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkit, tirukkaḻukkuṉṟil mīṇṭun tōṉṟum attaḻaluruvik kaṇṭiṟiñciya piṉṉart tillic ciṟṟampalam pōtuka! eṉak kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭu maṟittaruḷiṉaṉ. ataṉpiṟ cila nāṭkaḷ kāṟum aṭikaḷ ak kaṭṭaḷippaṭiyē āṅkut tavattiliruntu, ataṉ niṟivēṟṟatti etirpārttirukkiyil, orunāḷ ap poykiyiṉūṭē collaṟku ariya pēroḷiyōṭum ōr aṉaṟpiḻampu kiḷarntu tōṉṟi oḷira, ataṉik kaṇṭa aṭikaḷi yuḷḷiṭṭa aṭiyār kuḻāttuṭ palar ataṉkaṭ ceṉṟu vīḻntu maṟintaṉar. eñci niṉṟa aṭikaḷum maṟṟuñ cilarum at taḻaṟ piḻampik kaṇṭu toḻuturukiṉar. tamakku veḷippaṭṭu aruḷal vēṇṭumeṉak kuṟiyiranta aṭikaṭku iraṅki iṟivaṉ aḻal vaṭiviṟṟōṉṟiṉami,
emakku veḷippaṭā yeṉṉa viyaṉtaḻal
  veḷippaṭṭa entāy5
eṉṟu aṭikaḷē aruḷicceytavāṟṟāl teḷiyappaṭum. iṉi, aṭikaḷ maṭṭum eñci niṉṟamiyum ēṉi aṭiyavarkaḷellārum at taḻaliṟ pukuntu maṟintu iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi cērnta kuṟippum,
viccukkēṭu poykkākāteṉṟu
	iṅku eṉivittāy
  iccikku āṉār ellārum
	vantuṉ tāḷcērntār6
eṉavum,
aṭiyēṉ allēṉ kollōtāṉ
	eṉi āṭ koṇṭili kollō
  aṭiyārāṉār ellārum vantuṉ
	tāḷcērntār
eṉavum,
	civamānakar kuṟukap
  pōṉār aṭiyār yāṉum poyyum
	puṟamē pōntōmē
eṉavum,
pērā ulakam pukkār aṭiyār
	puṟamē pōntēṉ yāṉ
eṉavum.
aḻukēṉ niṉpāl aṉpām maṉamāy aḻalcērnta
  meḻukē yaṉṉār miṉṉār poṉṉār kaḻalkaṇṭu
  toḻutē uṉṉit toṭarntā rōṭun toṭarātē
  paḻutē piṟantēṉ eṉkoṇ ṭuṉṉip paṇikēṉē
eṉavum aṭikaḷē ōtumāṟṟāl uṇarappaṭum.
iṉi, moyyār taṭam poykiyiṟ kiḷarntu tōṉṟiya iṟivaṉatu taḻalvaṭivi aṭikaḷ kaṇṭu meḻukāyurukit toḻuta piṉṉart, taṉkaṭpukka aṭiyārōṭum avvaṉaṟpiḻampu maṟintatu. iṟivaṉ tamakkiṭṭa kaṭṭaḷiyi niṉintu aṭikaḷ kuruntamarattiṉ aṭiyip palakālum vīḻntiṟiñci āṟṟāmiyōṭum ataṉip pirintu, tirupperuntuṟiyi viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭut, tiruvuttarakōcamaṅkiyic ceṉṟu cērntār. aṅkē aṭikaḷ ciṉṉāḷ iruntu tavam iyaṟṟa, āṅkuḷḷatoru kuḷattil maṟittun taḻalvaṭivu kiḷarntu tōṉṟi viḷaṅka lāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu aṅkut tōṉṟiya taḻalvaṭiviṉiyē,
teṅkulavu cōlit tiruvuttarakōca maṅki
  taṅkulavu cōtit taṉiyuruvum vantaruḷi
  eṅkaḷ piṟappaṟut tiṭṭu7
eṉṟu aṭikaḷum ōtiyaruḷiṉār. uttarakōcamaṅkiyil aṭikaḷ tavattilirunta kālattu iṟivaṉ tamakku veḷippaṭutali vēṇṭik kuṟiyirantu pāṭiyatu nīttal viṇṇappam eṉṉum ceyyuṭ ṭokutiyē yātal, ataṉkaṇ mutal irupatu pāṭṭukkaḷil uttarakōcamaṅki eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭiruppatē cāṉṟām. iṉi, āṇṭu iṟivaṉ taḻaluruviṟ ṟōṉṟi avarkkuk kāṭci tantu aruḷiyapiṉ, aṭikaḷ pāṭiyatu tiruppoṉṉūcal eṉpatūum aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.  it, tirupapoṉ ṉūcal tilliyil aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭa teṉṟu evarō varintu vittatu ciṟitum poruntātu; eṉṉi? at tiruppoṉṉūcal oṉpatu ceyyuṭkaḷilum tiruvuttarakōca maṅkiyē vitanteṭuttu vittup pāṭappaṭutaliṉ eṉka. aṭikaḷ tiruvuttarakōca maṅkiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya taḻaṟpiḻampik kaṇṭu toḻutapiṉṉar, aṅkē tamatu tavam niṟivēṟap peṟṟār.
iṟivaṉ tamakkup paṇittaruḷiyavāṟē tiruvuttarakōca maṅkiyi viṭṭakaṉṟu, pāṇṭināṭṭiluḷḷa tirukkōyilkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkuñ ceṉṟu āṇṭāṇṭu iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkik koṇṭu, piṉ cōḻanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa tiruvārūri aṭintu civapirāṉit toḻutu tirut teḷḷēṇattil mutaṟ pattuppāṭṭum, tirumpulam palil mutaṟ pāṭṭum pāṭiyaruḷiṉār. iṅkē tiruvātavūrar purāṇak kūṟṟu māṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. pāṇṭināṭṭi viṭṭu  akaṉṟapiṉ aṭikaḷ mutaliṟ cōḻa nāṭṭil tiruviṭi marutūri aṭintār eṉavum, ataṟpiṟ ṟiruvārūri aṭintu āṇṭut tiruppulampal muṟṟum ōtiṉār eṉavum ap purāṇaṅ kūṟā niṉṟatu. maṟṟu maturiyiviṭṭu vaṭakku nōkkic cōḻa nāṭṭut tirukkōyilkaṭkuc celvār mutaliṟ ṟiru vārūri yaṭintē, ataṉpiṉ ataṟkum vaṭakkēyuḷḷa tiruviṭi marutūr cella vēṇṭutaliṉ ap purāṇattiṟ kūṟiya muṟi poruntāteṉṟuṇarntu koḷka. atuvēyu maṉṟit, tiruppulampal mūṉṟu pāṭṭukkaḷil mutaṟpāṭ ṭoṉṟumē tiruvārūr mēlatāyp pāṭappaṭṭiruppa tallāmal, iraṇṭām pāṭṭut tirupperuntuṟi mēlum mūṉṟām pāṭṭut tirukkuṟṟālattiṉ mēlum pāṭappaṭ ṭiruttaliṉ ivi mūṉṟum oru kālattē oruṅkē tiruvārūril aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟāl poruttamil uriyām.
ovvoru tirukkōyilil ovvorukāl oṉṟuñ cilavum palavumāy aṭikaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa pāṭṭukkaḷē piṉṉuḷḷōrāl tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟu tokuttum vakuttum vikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām nuṇṇiya ārāycciyuṭiyārkkaṉṟi ēṉiyōrkku oru ciṟitum viḷaṅkā. tirutteḷḷēṇattil piṟa pattup pāṭṭukkaḷ cilavaṟṟil tilliyampalaṅ kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ avi pattum āṇṭuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu peṟutum; mutaṟpattiṉ iraṇṭāñ ceyyuḷil tiruvārūr kūṟappaṭ ṭiruttaliṉ avi pattum āṇṭuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu peṟutum; aṟṟāyiṉ, avvattirukkōyiṟ peyar maṭṭum uṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ maṭṭumē āṇṭāṇṭuc ceyyap paṭṭaṉa, ēṉiya allaveṉak koḷḷāmōveṉiṟ; koḷḷalām. oru tirukkōyilip pāṭuṅkāl ataṉmēṟ perumpālum pattuc ceyyuṭkaḷ iyaṟṟutal tollāciriyar muṟiyāyp pōtaliṉ, tiruvārūrp peyar colliya ceyyuḷ oṉṟāyiruppiṉum ataṉō ṭiyinta ēṉi oṉpatu tirutteḷḷēṇac ceyyuṭkaḷum akkōyiliṉ mēlaṉavākavē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāku meṉak kōṭal iḻukkātu.
iṅṅaṉamē ataṉ piṟpattuc ceyyuṭkaḷum tilli yampalattiṉ mēlavātal koḷḷappaṭum iṉit, tiruvēcaṟavu oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷil,
 aruḷ eṉakku iṅku iṭimarutē
  iṭaṅkoṇṭa ammāṉē.
eṉṟu tiruviṭimarutūr kūṟappaṭṭirutataliṉ ac ceyyuṭkaḷ pattum aṅkē aruḷicceyyappaṭṭami peṟappaṭum; aṟṟēl ataṉ ēḻāñ ceyyuḷil,
 teṉpālit tirupperuntuṟi
  yuṟiyuñ civaperumāṉ.
eṉṟurikkappaṭṭa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ; tamakku iṟivaṉ āṇṭuk kuruvaṭiyil eḻuntaruḷi aruḷceytataṉi niṉin turitta attuṇiyē yallatu, iṅku iṭimarutē iṭaṅkoṇṭa ammāṉē eṉṉum ataṉ oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷiṟ pōlak kūṟāmiyiṉ, appatikam tirupperun tuṟiyiṟ ceytatākāteṉ ṟuṇarntu koḷka. iṉi, aṭikaḷ tiruviṭimarutūri viṭṭakaṉṟu tirukkaḻumalam eṉṉum cīrkāḻiyi aṭintu āṇṭu iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkiṉār. ivviṭattē piṭittapattu aruḷicceytār eṉṟa tiruvātavūrar purāṇaṅ kūṟum. āṉāl, ap piṭittapattup pāṭṭukkaḷil eṅkuñ cīrkāḻiyip paṟṟiya kuṟippu ētuṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyāl avi aṅkē tāṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟal iyalātatāyirukkiṉṟatu. cilanāṭ cīrkāḻiyil viki, ataṉpiṉ tilliyampalam irukkum mūlinōkki vaṇaṅkikkoṇṭu, tamakku iṟivaṉ paṇittavāṟē aṭikaḷ tirukkaḻukkuṉṟañ cellumvaḻi mēṟ koḷvārāṉār. av vaḻiyiṉiṭiyē tirumutukuṉṟam, tiruveṇṇey nallūr eṉṉun tiruppatikaḷiyuñ ceṉṟu aṭikaḷ vaṇaṅkiṉāreṉat tiruvātavūrar purāṇaṅ kūṟumēṉum, ak kōyilkaḷiṉ peyarēṉum kuṟippēṉum tiruvācakan tirukkōviyāril eṅkuṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ avvuri koḷḷaṟpālataṉṟeṉka.
maṟṟut tirukkaḻukkuṉṟam nōkkic cellum avvaḻiyiṉiṭiyē aṭikaḷ tiruvaṇṇāmali ceṉṟu, aṅkē iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkiyuṟiyum nāṭkaḷil, mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷ vantatu. aṅkē civapirāṉ kōyiliṟ ṟiruttoṇṭu ceyyum mātarkaḷ attiṅkaḷiṉ pularikkāliyilē tuyil nīṅkiyeḻuntu, aṅṅaṉam muṉ eḻātu iṉṉun tuyililirukkun tammiṉattavaril maṟṟiyari avar kūṟumāṟākac civa
pirāṉṟaṉ aruṭṭiṟaṅkaḷip pāṭa viḻintu, avariyun, tammōṭuṭaṉ pōnta tam maṉiviyār avarkkup pāṅ kāyiṉār mutaliyōriyum muṉṉilippaṭuttit tiruvem pāvi aruḷic ceytār. iḵtu it tirukkōyi liṉmēl aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku, itaṉ patiṉeṭṭāñ ceyyuḷil aṇṇāmaliyāṉ aṭikkamalam eṉṟu pōnta kuṟippē cāṉṟām. iḵtu ak kōyiṟ ṟiruttoṇṭu puriyum mātari nōkkic collappaṭṭamikku ataṉ pattāñ ceyyuḷiṟ kōtil kulattāṉṟaṉ kōyiṟ piṇāp piḷḷikāḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷi viḷittu aṭikaḷē aruḷicceytamiyē cāṉṟām. iṉi, ataṉ patiṉintām ceyyuḷil,
 ōrorukāl emperumāṉ eṉṟeṉṟē namperumāṉ
  cīrorukāl vāyōvāḷ cittaṅ kaḷikūra
  nīrorukāl ōvā neṭuntāri kaṇpaṉippap
  pārorukāl vantaṉiyāḷ viṇṇōrit tāṉpaṇiyāḷ
  pērariyarkku iṅṅaṉē pittoruvar āmāṟu
eṉṟu aṭikaḷ ōtutali uyttu nōkkuṅkāl, avar itaṉkaṭṭam maṉiviyār civapirāṉmāṭṭu vitta pēraṉpiṉ ṟiṟatti viyantu pāṭiṉāreṉṟal iḻukkātu. itaṉōṭu, āṇṭukkōyiliṟ ṟoṇṭu puriyum an naṅkimār viḷiyāṭiya ammāṉi viḷiyāṭṭik kaṇṭu, ataṉālum avarkaḷ civapirāṉ aruṭṭiṟaṅkaḷil niṉivip patittup pēriṉpattiṟ paṭital vēṇṭit, tiruvammāṉi irupatu ceyyuṭkaḷum aruḷicceytār. it tiruvammāṉi aṇṇā
malik kaṇṇētāṉ aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭa teṉpatu, ataṉ pattāñ ceyyuḷil aṇṇāmaliyāṉip pāṭutuṅ kāṇ ammāṉāy, eṉṟu aṭikaḷē kūṟiyavāṟṟāḷ pōtarum. iṉi, aṭikaḷ tiruvaṇṇāmalikku eḻuntaruḷiyatum mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷ vantateṉpatāl, avar āvaṇit tiṅkaḷ mūlanāḷukkup piṉ maturiyi viṭṭakaṉṟu ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ variyil iṭiyiṭiyēyuḷḷa tirukkōyilkaṭkuc ceṉṟu āṇṭāṇṭu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu vantāreṉpatu peṟappaṭum eṉka.
iṉi, aṭikaḷ tiruvaṇṇāmaliyi viṭṭakaṉṟu tirukkacciyēkampam cērntu, āṇṭu ammi taḻuvak kuḻinta perumāṉi yētti, aṅkē unti eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭip puriyum makaḷirik kaṇṭu avarkūṟum paricākat tiruvunti yār aruḷicceytār. iḵtu aṅkē aruḷicceyyappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku,
īrampu kaṇṭilam ēkampar taṅkiyil
eṉṟu ataṉ iraṇṭāñ ceyyuḷil ēkampar eṉṉum peyar kūṟappaṭṭiruttalē cāṉṟām. it tiruvuntiyār tilliyil aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭateṉṟu urippataṟkuc cāṉṟu appāṭṭukkaḷil eṅkuṅ kaṇṭilam. aṟṟēl, tiruppoṟ cuṇṇam nāṉkām pāṭṭiṟ kaccit tiruvēkampaṉ cempoṟ kōyil pāṭi eṉṟu it tirukkōyil kuṟikkappaṭṭirutta lāṉum, aṅṅaṉamē tiruppūvalli patiṉāṉkām pāṭṭil ēkampam mēyapirāṉ eṉṟu it tirukkōyilil ēḻuntaruḷiyirukkuñ civapirāṉ peyar kūṟappaṭṭiruttalāṉum aviyiraṇṭuṅ kacciyēkampattilētāṉ aruḷic ceyyap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ; tiruppoṟcuṇṇam mutaṟpāṭṭil, attaṉ aiyāṟaṉ eṉṉun tiruviyāṟṟup perumāṉ peyarum, ataṉ oṉpatām pāṭṭil
aṇitilli vāṇaṉukkē
eṉavum, patiṉōrām pāṭṭil,
ampalat tāṭi ṉāṉukkum
eṉavum, pattoṉpatām pāṭṭil,
tillipāṭic ciṟṟampalat teṅkaḷ celvam pāṭi
eṉavun tillipperumāṉ peyarum pōtarak kāṇṭaliṉ, perumpāṉmipaṟṟit tiruppoṟcuṇṇam tilliyil aruḷic ceyyappaṭṭateṉṟum, aṅṅaṉamē tiruppūvalli mutaṟpāṭṭu iraṇṭām pāṭṭu ēḻāmpāṭṭup patiṉāṉkām pāṭṭukkaḷil tillimpalamum tillip patikkaracuṅ kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ at tiruppūvalliyum avvāṟē tilliyil aruḷicceyyappaṭṭa teṉṟuṅ koḷḷutalē poruttamām eṉka.
ataṉpiṉ, aṭikaḷ tirukkacciyēkampattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkit tirukkaḻukkuṉṟam vantu iṟivaṉit toḻutu tavattil vikiṉār. avvāṟirukkunāḷil avarkku muṉṉē civapirāṉ taṉatu aruḷoḷi vaṭiviṉik kāṭṭa, aṭikaḷ ataṉik kaṇṭu pēriṉpa veḷḷattu aḻuntiṉarāyp piṇikkilāta eṉpataṉi mutalāka uṭiya tirukkaḻuk kuṉṟap patikac ceyyuṭkaḷ ēḻum aruḷic ceytaṉar. ac ceyyuḷ ovvoṉṟaṉ īṟṟilum iṟivaṉ taṉatu tirukkōlattit tamakkuk kāṭṭiyapaṭiyi aṭikaḷē aruḷicceytiruttal kāṇka. īṇṭu aṭikaḷ kaṇṭa kōlam aṉaṟpiḻampu vaṭivē eṉpatu emakku veḷippaṭā yeṉṉa viyaṉtaḻal veḷippaṭṭa entāy eṉṟu avar tāmē aruṭpattil ōtumāṟupaṟṟit tuṇiyappaṭum. av vaṭivilaṉṟit, tirupperuntuṟiyil mutaṟkaṭpōnta kuruvaṭivilēyē īṇṭum iṟivaṉ avarkkuk kāṭci tantaruḷiṉāṉeṉṟu koḷḷāmō veṉiṟ, koḷḷām; eṉṉi? mutaṟkaṭ kuruvaṭiviṟ pōntu tammi aṭimi koṇṭa aiyaṉatu av vuruvattiyē īṇṭuṅ kaṇṭaṉarāyiṉ ataṉpāl aṉpumītūrappeṟṟa avar attiruvuruvattiṉ iyalpi ip patikattiṟ kiḷanteṭuttuk kūṟātu irār ākaliṉ eṉka.
piṉṉar aṭikaḷ tirukkaḻukkuṉṟatti āṟṟāmiyōṭum viṭṭakaṉṟa, iṟivaṉ paṇitta kaṭṭaḷippaṭiyē tilli mūtūr vantu cērntār. aṅkē tirucciṟṟampalattil iṭiyaṟātu tirukkūttiyaṟṟum civa
pirāṉṟaṉ iṉpavuruviṉik kaṇṭu kaṇṇīrvāra meymmayir poṭippap peruṅ kaḷippuṟṟu intiriya vayamayaṅki eṉpataṉi mutalāka uṭiya kaṇṭapattu aruḷic ceytār. ataṉpiṟ poṉṉampalatti valaṅ
koṇṭu ceṉṟu, pulikkāl muṉivaṉum nākamum vaḻi
paṭṭa iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkip pōy, at tilli nakariṉ puṟattē yuḷḷatoru kāṭṭil tavattil ōviyampōl acivaṟṟiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam paṉṉāḷ tavattil iruppatum, avvāṟu akattē iṟivaṉ aruṭperukkil paṭinta tamatu meyyuṇarvu ōrōvorukāṟ puṟattē tirumpiyavaḻit tiruvācakac ceḻumpāṭalkaḷ ōtuvatumāka aṭikaḷ akappuṟamiraṇṭiṉuñ civapirāṉatu aruṭperun tiruvuruviṟ ṟōynta meyyaṉpiṉ uruvāṉār.


aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	cettilāp pattu, (2)
2.	 tiruccatakam, (42)
3.	 tiruccatakam, (93)
4.	tiruccatakam, (84)
5.	aruṭ pattu, (4)
6.	tiruccatakam, (81, 83, 87, 88) 
7.	tiruppoṉūñcal, (9)


9. puttaroṭu
coṟpēr toṭaṅkutal
ītivvāṟirukkac, civapirāṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ iṭiyaṟā aṉpuperukun tiruttavam uṭiyār oruvar, cōḻanāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa tilli mutalāṉa tirukkōyil kaḷit toḻutāṉapiṉ, paḻutillā īḻanāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa tirukkētīccaram tirukkōṇamali mutalāṉa tirukkōyil kaḷiyum vaṇaṅkal vēṇṭi aṅkē ceṉṟār. ceṉṟavar aṅkē cellum iṭaṅkaṭōṟum poṉṉampalam poṉṉampalam eṉṟē collikkoṇṭu celvārāṉār. akkālattē ilaṅkit tīvāṉatu putta camayattavaṉāṉa ōr aracaṉ āḷukikkuḷ iruntatu. puttacamayattit taḻuviṉa kuṭimakkaḷuma an nāṭeṅkum paraviyiruntaṉar. ap putta camayattavarkaḷiṭiyē ac civaṉaṭiyār poṉṉampalam eṉṉuñ colli ōvātu uritta paṭiyāyc cellavē, avaratu civavaṭivattik kaṇṭum, avar kūṟum poṉṉampalam eṉṉuñ collik kēṭṭum maṉam puḻuṅkiya puttak kurumār cilar tam aracaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu akkumāli pūṇṭu nīṟupūci aiyam ēṟṟu uṇpāṉākiya civaṉ oruvaṉ niṟkumpōtum irukkum pōtum ekkālum poṉṉampalam eṉṟu colliyapaṭiyāy ulavu
kiṉṟaṉaṉ, eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. accoṟkēṭṭa am maṉṉavaṉ, avaṉi iṅkē koṇarvīrāka! eṉṟu tūtuvarkkuk kaṭṭaḷi tantāṉ. aṅṅaṉamē, avar ceṉṟu avvaṭiyāri aracaṉpāl aḻippa, ataṟku avar oṉṟi vēṇṭutalum, maṟṟoṉṟi veṟuttalum iṉṟi nāṭōṟum aiyam ēṟṟu uṇṭal oṉṟallatu vēṟētuṅ karutāta empōlvār māṭṭum aracarkku āvatu ētum uḷatō? eṉṟu maṟutturittār. mēlum, attūtuvar nīr ūril aiyam ēṟṟu uṇṭālum, umakkup piṟitoru muyaṟci illi
yāṉālum, taṉatu nāṭṭil iruppār ellāriyuṅ kāttal maṉṉaṟkuk kaṭaṉātalāl, em aracaṉ viruppappaṭi nīr varutal vēṇṭum, eṉṟu moḻintu mīṇṭum aḻippa, avvaṭiyārum ataṟku icintu avaruṭaṉ ēkiṉār.
tūtuvar avari araṇmaṉiyiṉuḷḷē aḻittup pōyp, putta kuruviṉuṭaṉ attāṇimaṇṭapattē viki yirukkum maṉṉavaṉ etirē viṭuttār. am mātavarum avvaracaṉ arukē ceṉṟu poṉṉampalam eṉak kūṟiya māntār. ac coṟkēṭṭa ap puttamaṉṉaṉ avvaṭiyāri nōkki, ippōtu nīr poṉṉampalam eṉak kūṟiyatu eṉṉi? eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ. ataṟku avar cōḻa aracarāṟ ceṅkōl celuttap paṭuṅ kāviri nāṭṭiṉkaṇ tilliyampalam eṉṟu oṉṟu uḷatu. atuvē innilavulakattil mutaṟkaṇ uṇṭākiya tirukkōyilākum; in nilavuṭam piṟku neñcat tāmaripōl vayaṅkāniṉṟa attiruk kōyiliṉuṭ poṉṉampalam eṉa oṉṟuḷatu; ivvuṭam piṉakattē ellā uyirkaḷiṉ neñcattāmariyiṉ veḷiyil iṟivaṉatu iyakkamāṉatu naṭipeṟṟu ivvuṭampiṉ kaṇ uyir ulavutaṟku utavutalpōla uyirōṭu kūṭiya iv vuṭampukaḷ ulavutaṟ poruṭṭum in nilavuṭampi nilipeṟuttuvāṉ vēṇṭi iṟivaṉ ataṉ neñcam pōlvatākiya poṉṉampalattilē iṭiyaṟātu āṭutalceytu utavipurikiṉṟāṉ. maṉuviṉ mintaṉ oruvaṉ koṇṭa toḻunōyi māṟṟi avaṉatu uṭampiṉip poṉpōl oḷiracceyta civakaṅki yeṉṉun tiruk kuḷamum at tirukkōyiliṉuḷ uṇṭu; at tirukkuḷattiṉ kaṇ meyyaṉpuṭaṉ mūḻkiyeḻuntu poṉṉampalattē aintoḻil aruṭkūttu iyaṟṟā niṉṟa iṟivaṉṟaṉ ṟiruvuruvi uḷḷam nīrāy urukit toḻuvārkku iv vulakil mīṇṭum piṟantu varuntā vīṭṭuneṟi eḷitiṟ kiṭṭum. ittuṇip peruñciṟappuṭiyatāṉa poṉṉampalatti orukāṟ collum; atu, civa eṉun tirumaṟiyi irupattōrāyirattu aṟunūṟumuṟi ceppi uruvēṟṟiyataṟku oppāṉa perumpayaṉit tarum, eṉṟu moḻintiṭṭār.
ic coṟkaḷi aracaṉarukirunta putta kuru kēṭṭup peritum vekuṇṭu, piṭakanūl mūṉṟum uriceyta em talivaṉākiya cākkiyamuṉiva ṉallātu vēṟumoru kaṭavuḷ uṇṭō! ippōtē yām tillinakar cērntu, aṅkuḷḷa civaroṭu vaḻakkiṭṭu, ap poṉṉampalattil āṭuṅ kūttaṉatu aṭikaḷiṟ ṟoṭutta kaḻaliyum mēleṭutta āṉēṟṟiṉ koṭiyiyum aṟuttup, putta muṉivaṉ oruvaṉē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉa ulakōr aṟiya uriceytu, ammaṉṟatti araciṉ nīḻaṟ perumāṉukkup paḷḷiyāka ākkuvēm. icceyali mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷil muṭikkak
kaṭavēm, eṉa vañciṉam pukaṉṟu, tillikkēkat toṭaṅkiṉār. ap putta aracaṉun taṉ makaṭkuḷḷa ūmit taṉmiyāṉatu tillip poṉṉampalañ cella nīṅkumeṉak kōṇūl kūṟiṉami terintāṉākaliṉ, tāṉun taṉ makaḷōṭum ilaṅkiviṭṭum puṟappaṭṭu at tillinakar vantu cērntāṉ;
puttakuru tillit tirukkōyiliṉuḷḷē taṉṉaraca ṉōṭuñ ceṉṟu oru maṇṭapattiṉmī tamarntirukka, atukaṇṭu akkōyilil tiruttoṇṭu ceyvārellām avarpāl vantu kuḻumi, ōputtakuruvē! nīr iṅkiruttal ākātu. it tillinakar elliyi uṭaṉē kaṭantu cellutalē takkatu, eṉṟu urippa, ataṟku ap puttakuru, cōḻavēntaṉ muṉṉiliyil uyarnta aḷavi neṟiyāl, nīvir taḻuviyoḻukum civam meymiyuṭit taṉṟeṉa maṟuttu, eṅkaḷ kutamaputtarē mutaṟkaṭavuḷ eṉpati niliniṟuttiya piṉṉallatu itaṉiviṭṭu yām ēkuva
tilli, eṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉār.
ivvāṟu puttakuru kūṟutalikkēṭṭa ak kūṭṭattiṉar civapirāṉukku vaḻipāṭu āṟṟuṅ kōyil antaṇa riṭattum, civanūluṇarcci yuṭiyāriṭattum pōy naṭantavikaḷi eṭuttuccolla, avarellām ciṉat tōṭum apputtakuruviṉarukē pōntu, nīr accamiṉṟi iṅkē vantami ēṉ? eṉṟu viṉava, ataṟkavar, nuṅkaḷ camayanūṟpaṭi nuṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷē mutaṟkaṭavuḷ eṉṟu nīṅkaḷ niliniṟuttuvīrkaḷāyiṉ. ataṉi maṟuttu, ematu camaya nūṟpaṭi puttakkaṭavuḷē mutaṟkaṭavuḷ eṉṟu nilināṭṭuvēm; ātalāl, emmōṭu vaḻakkiṭa vammiṉkaḷ! eṉṟu aṟikūvi aḻittār. ataṉmēṟ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu ak kuruvi vita antaṇarkaḷ piṟaku teḷivaṭintu, puttaṉ viṉāviyavaṟṟiṟku viṭikūṟa aṟiyāmal avaṉi vitār aṭittār eṉṟu ulakam nammip paḻikkum; ātalāṟ cōḻa vēntaṉ muṉṉiliyil oru pēravi kūṭṭi yām vaḻakkiṭṭāl takkatitu, takātatitu veṉṟu ētoru kalakamum naṭavāmal muṭikkalām, eṉṟu tammuḷ oru tīrmāṉattukku vantu, piṉṉar, nikaḻntavikaḷiyum tamatu tīrmāṉattiyum viritteḻutic cōḻa maṉṉaṉukku oru tirumukamum, iṅṅaṉamē teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa ēṉi maṉṉarkkum kaṟṟārkkum aruntavattōrkkum palappala tirumukaṅkaḷum pōkkit, tattam maṉiyakattē ceṉṟu, aṉṟiravu, periyatoru maṉakkavaliyōṭun tuyil koḷḷalāṉār.
avvāṟu avarellām tuyilkiyil, avarellārkkuṅ kaṉaviṉ kaṇṇē, taḻitta caṭimuṭiyum nīṟu polinta tirumēṉiyum pirappaṅkōl tāṅkiya kiyum uṭiya aḻakiya ōr aruntavar uruvantōṉṟalāyiṟṟu; tōṉṟiya at tiruvuruvam vāy tiṟantu, nīvir maṉaṅkalaṅkutal oḻimiṉ! ivvūr ellippuṟattē vātavūraṉ eṉṉum oru muṉivaṉ aruntavattil amarntiruk
kiṉṟāṉ; am muṉivaṉi aḻittuvaruvīrēl avaṉ aḷavi nūṉmuṟi vaḻuvātē puttarōṭu vaḻakkiṭṭu avari velvāṉ, eṉṟu urittu maṟintu pōyiṟṟu.
viyakkattakka vakiyāy nikaḻnta ik kaṉavu kaṇṭu viḻitta antaṇar ellām iṟivaṉit toḻuteḻuntu, pularik kāliyilē kōyilil oru maṇṭapattē vantukūṭit tāṅtāṅ kaṇṭa kaṉavu nikaḻcciyiṉi eṭuttukkūṟit, tam mellārkkun tōṉṟiya tiruvuruvamum aḵtu urittaṉavum muṟṟum ottiruttal kaṇṭu mika viyappuṟṟu, aiyaṉaruḷi vāḻtti maṉakkalakkan tīrntu, ellārumāy oruṅkē tiruvātavūraṭikaḷ irukkum aṭavinōkkic ceṉṟaṉar. aṭikaḷō puṟattuṇarcci ciṟitum illārāyc civapirāṉ ṟiruvaṭikkaṇ muḻutum patinta niṉiviṉarāy iruppa, avari aḻikkac ceṉṟa antaṇarkaḷ avaratu acivaṟṟa tavaniliyiṉiyum, avar tamatu varukiyiyum uṇarā rāyiruttaliyuṅ kaṇṭu nirampa maṉam varuntit tattam mayikañceṉṟu aṉṟiravu tuyil koḷkiyil muṉṉāṭ kaṉaviṟ ṟōṉṟiya tavavuruvam innāṭkaṉaviṉun tōṉṟi ammuṉivaṉi māṇikkavācakaṉ eṉṉum peyar colli aḻimiṉkaḷ! eṉṟu moḻintu maṟintatu. atu kaṇṭu viḻitta antaṇar ellārum mīṇṭum avvaṭavi nōkkic ceṉṟu ap peyar colli aḻippa, uṭaṉē aṭikaḷ akattē niṉivu oruṅkiyanili kalintu viḻittut, tampāl vanta avvantaṇar vēṇṭukōḷukku icintu ava
ruṭaṉ pōntu, ampalakkūttaṉi vaṇaṅkiyeḻuntu, putta kuruvum avar tam kuḻāttiṉarum oruṅku kūṭiyirunta maṇṭapattiṉkaṇ ēṟi, antaṇaruṅ kaṟṟārun tuṟavōrun tammip puṭicūḻa, āṇṭu iṭappaṭṭa ōr irukki mītu amarntaruḷiṉār.
appōtu, antaṇarkaḷāl muṉṉarē tirumukam viṭuttu aḻikkappaṭṭa cōḻamaṉṉaṉum vantu avvavik kaḷattē pukuntu aṭikaḷiṉ tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki yeḻuntu, avarāṟ paṇikkappaṭṭavāṟē aṅkiṭappaṭṭa tōr aṇimēl amarntaṉaṉ. aṅku ataṟku muṉṉarē vantirunta īḻa maṉṉaṉ accōḻaṉukku aṭaṅki aracu purivōṉātaliṉ avaṉi vaṇaṅki avaṉatu kaṭṭaḷi peṟṟut taṉṉirukkiyil iruntāṉ. irutiṟattār vaḻakkukaḷiyum kēṭṭu naṭu niṉṟa cāṉṟukūṟuvārāṉa cāṉṟōrum vantu tāmirukkumiṭattē vīṟṟiruntaṉar. varutaṟkuriyārellām vantu niṟintu appēravi oḻuṅkupeṟṟapiṉ, cōḻa vēntaṉ eḻuntu aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkit tēvarīr putta kuru kūṟuṅ koḷkikaḷi maṟuttuc civak koḷkiyiṉi niliniṟuttutaṟkut tiruvuḷampaṟṟuvīrāka! eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. avvēṇṭukōḷukku oruppaṭṭa tiruvātavūraṭikaḷ puttakuruvi nōkki, numatu kaṭavuḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum, ak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭiyic cērum vīṭṭiṉilakkaṇamum nīvir eṭutturimiṉ! eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
ataṟku ap puttakuru, ellāvaṟṟiyum muṟṟum aṟintu, koli kaḷavu poy kaḷ kāmam eṉṉum aivakik kuṟṟamuṅ kaṭintu, paḻutillā aruṭperuntaṉmi yiṉālē pala piṟavikaḷilum piṟanta avvavvuyirt tuṉpamum poṟāṉāy at tuṉpaṅkaḷit tāṉ ēṉṟu koṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku naṉmi ceytu, vinayapiṭakam cattapiṭakam api taṉmapiṭakam eṉṉum vaḻuvillāta mūṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷiyum aruḷicceytu aracamaranīḻalil eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum putta muṉivaṉē em iṟivaṉāvāṉ eṉṟu aṟimiṉ! iṉi, uruvam vētaṉi kuṟippup pāvaṉi viññāṉam eṉṉum aivakik kantakamum oruṅku tokkatokiyē uṭampum uyirum āvataṉṟi, iviyintiṉ vēṟāka uṭampum uyirum eṉat taṉittu iru poruḷkaḷ illi. ik kantaṅkaḷuḷ etuvum uyiraṉṟu eṉpatu emmuṭiya piṭakanūlkaḷil maṟittum maṟittum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṭampum ēṉip poruḷkaḷum iṭiyaṟātu māṟikkoṇṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. muṟ kaṇattirunta atē āṇmakaṉ piṟkaṇattu uḷḷavaṉ allaṉ; avaṉiṭattil eṉṟum nilipēṟāy uḷḷatu etuvum illi. ivvivakik kantaṅkaḷum oruṅku tokkavaḻi yāṉ oruvaṉ uḷaṉ eṉṉum uṇarcci tōṉṟā niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvintuṅ kūṭiya toki aḻintavaḻi yāṉ uḷaṉ eṉṉum uṇarvum aḻiyā niṟkum. iv vintiṉ kūṭṭamuṅ kalivum aṟiyā tirukkuṅkāṟum viṉiyāṟ piṟaviyuḷatām; avi yuṇarntavaḻi meyyuṇarvu tōṉṟip piṟavi illiyām; ituvē vīṭupēṟām eṉṟu teḷimiṉ! iṉi, iṅṅaṉam ivi ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mutalākiya uruvam vētaṉi kuṟippup pāvaṉi viññāṉam eṉṉuṅ kantaṅkaḷintum yāviyeṉpirēl, avi tammiyum oru ciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭutum: uruvak kantamāvatu poruḷkaḷiṉ uruvum ap poruṭ paṇpukaḷiṉ uruvum eṉa iruvakittāy ovvoṉṟum nannāṉkām; poruḷkaḷiṉ uru; maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl eṉa  nāṉkām; apporuṭ paṇpukaḷiṉ uru: vaṉmi cuvi oḷi nāṟṟam eṉa nāṉkām; vētaṉik kantamāvatu iṉpamun tuṉpamum avviraṇṭum iṉmiyum eṉa mūṉṟām; kuṟippuk kantamāvatu mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi yeṉṉum aimpoṟikaḷum avaṟṟip porunti niṟkum maṉamum eṉa āṟām. pāvaṉik kantamāvatu, maṉam moḻi mey eṉṉum mūṉṟiyum paṟṟi nikaḻum nalviṉi pattum tīviṉi pattumeṉa irupatām, avaṟṟuḷ nalviṉi pattāvaṉa; aruḷ avāviṉmi tava viruppam eṉa maṉattāṉ nikaḻum mūṉṟum, iṉiyavi kūṟal meymmi kūṟal payaṉuḷakūṟal aṟamurittal eṉa moḻiyāṉ nikaḻum nāṉkum, vaṇakkam īki tavampurital eṉa meyyāṇ nikaḻum mūṉṟum ām; tīviṉi pattāvaṉa: tīyaṉa niṉittal avā vekuḷi eṉa maṉattāṉ nikaḻum mūṉṟum, kaṭuñcol poykūṟal payaṉila collal kōṭcollal eṉa moḻiyāṉ nikaḻum nāṉkum, kaḷavu koli vīṇ ceyki eṉa meyyāṉ nikaḻum ām. viññāṉa kantamāvatu aimpoṟikaḷiyum maṉattiyum paṟṟi nikaḻum āṟaṟivukaḷām eṉṟu ivvāṟu uṇarntu koṇmiṉ! eṉṟu viṭi kūṟiṉār.

10. putta camayak
kōṭpāṭukaḷi maṟuttal
ataṉmēl tiruvātavūraṭikaḷ ap puttakuru kūṟiyavikaḷi niralē maṟuppārāy piṉvarumāṟu uri nikaḻttuvārāṉār; muṟkaṇattil iruntatu piṟkaṇattil illiyām eṉpatē numatu kōṭpāṭātalāl, num puttamuṉivaṉ orukaṇattil aṟinta aṟivum ataṟku aṭutta kaṇattil illiyām eṉṟē numatu kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi muṭikkappaṭum; avvāṟu muṭikkappaṭavē, muṟkaṇattu nikaḻnta aṟivu māyntu pōkap pōkap piṟkaṇattu avaṉ putitu putitāka aṟitalē nikaḻāniṟkum. ataṉāl avaṟku nikaḻkāla aṟivē tōṉṟit tōṉṟi māyumallatu, iṟantakāla aṟivum ak kālattu nikaḻ poruḷiṉaṟivum avaṟku ilavātal vēṇṭum. allatūum, orukaṇattu maṭṭumē nikaḻum aṟivu ataṉ piṟkaṇaṅkaḷil nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟi aṟiyāta tākal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam aṟiyātākavē avaṟku varuṅkāla aṟivum ilatātal peṟappaṭum. iṉik kaṇaṅkaṭōṟun tōṉṟum nikaḻ kālavaṟivum nilipēṟiṉṟi māyntoḻitaliṉ avvaṟivu nikaḻcciyāṟ pōnta payaṉum oṉṟum iṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam mukkāla aṟivu nikaḻccikkum oru toṭarppāṭu numatu koḷkiyiṉkaṇ iṉmiyiṉ, num puttamuṉivaṉ ellā
vaṟṟiyum oruṅkuṇarvaṉ eṉpatu poruḷil kūṟṟēyām. nēṟṟu itaṉic ceytavaṉ yāṉē eṉa iṟanta kālavuṇarvum, iṉṟu itaṉic ceykiṉṟēṉ eṉa nikaḻkāla uṇarvum, nāḷi itaṉic ceytu muṭippēṉ eṉa etirkāla vuṇarvum, oru toṭarpāka nikaḻappeṟum makkaḷukkuḷḷa attuṇi yaṟivutāṉum numatu kaṭavuḷukku iṉmiyiṉ, aḵtu ellā aṟivum mutaṉmiyum uṭiya muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷātal yāṇṭiyateṉṟoḻika!
iṉi, num iṟivaṉ piṟavuyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpam poṟāṉāy av vuyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpattit tāṉ ēṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭit tāṉ palluyirkaḷāyp piṟavi yeṭuppaṉeṉṟatūum, avaṉ koli mutalāya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ kaṭintō ṉeṉṟatūum poruntāmi kāṭṭuvām. ōruyirkku vanta tuṉpatti nīkkaluṟuvōṉ tāṉ attuṉpattiṟ paṟṟappaṭātavaṉāy iruntālaṉṟi attuṉpatti nīkka māṭṭuvāṉ allaṉ. kaṇṇiḻanta oru kuruṭaṉukku utavi ceyyavallavaṉ, kaṭpārvi naṉkuṭiya maṟṟoruvaṉē yallāmal avaṉpōṟ kaṇṇillāta vēṟu oru kuruṭaṉ allaṉē. avvāṟirukkap piṟavittuṉpattiṟ kiṭantuḻalum uyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpattit tuṭikkal vēṇṭi numatu kaṭavuḷum avvuyirkaḷippōṟ pala piṟavikaḷi yeṭuttāṉeṉṟu nummaṉōr kūṟuvatu, valiyil akappaṭṭut tuṭikkum pala māṉkaḷik kaṇṭa maṟṟorumāṉ avaṟṟiṉ tuṉpatti nīkkutaṟporuṭṭut tāṉum av valiyiṟ ceṉṟu akappaṭṭukkoṇṭataṉiyē okkum. aṟṟaṉṟu, uyirkaḷ, tammi yaṟiyāmalē piṟavikaḷiṟ kiṭantuḻaṉṟu tuṉpuṟum, em puttamuṉivaṉō taṉṉuṇarvum taṉvalimiyum muṟṟum uṭiyaṉāy avvuyirkaḷi ṉiṭiyē piṟantu avaṟṟiṉ tuṉpattip pōkkuvāṉ eṉṟurippirēl, avvāṟu ellā aṟivum āṟṟalum uṭiyāṉ avvuyirkaḷpōṟ piṟaviyeṭāmalē avaṟṟiṉ tuyar kaḷiya vallaṉātal vēṇṭumaṉṟi, avarpōṟ ṟāṉum piṟavi yaṭuttuḻalal eṟṟukku? mēlum, viṉinukarcciyiṉ poruṭṭākavē piṟavi varumeṉpatu nummaṉōrkkum uṭaṉpāṭākaliṉ, piṟavi yeṭutta uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟāy niṟkum num puttamuṉivaṉi avviṉiyiṉ nīṅkiya iṟivaṉākak kōṭal yāṅṅaṉam? viṉiyilum piṟaviyiluṅ kūṭi niṟpaṉāyiṉum em putta muṉivaṉ ellāmvalla iṟivaṉē yāvaṉ eṉṟu poruntāvaḻakkup pēcuvirāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē viṉiyilum piṟaviyiluṅ kūṭiniṟkum ovvōruyiriyum iṟivaṉākak koḷvōm eṉpārkku nīr viṭipakarumāṟu iṉṟām;
iṉṟākavē, num puttamuṉivaṉ uyirkaḷuḷ oruvaṉē yāvaṉallāl, avaṉ mutaṟkaṭavuḷātal cellā teṉṟuṇarka, aṟṟaṉṟu, uyirkaḷ irakkattāṟ piṟantā rallar, em puttamuṉivaṉō irakkattāṟ piṟantā ṉeṉpirēl, num puttaṉ piṟanta ñāṉṟu taṉ aṉṉiyiṉ valatu vilāppuṟattip piḷantukoṇṭu piṟakka, ataṉāl avaṉi īṉṟāḷ cila nāḷellāṅ kuṟṟuyirāyk kiṭantu iṟantāḷ eṉa nīvirē kūṟutaliṉ, taṉ aṉṉiyiṉ tuṉpat tiṟkum cāviṟkum ētuvāy vanta avaṉ irakkamuṭiyaṉātal yāṅṅaṉam? atuvēyumaṉṟi, num muṉivaṉ nariyum puliyum arimāvumākap piṟantavaḻi vēṟu melvilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟu tiṉṟāṉātal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? ataṉālum avaṉ irakka muṭiyāṉeṉṟaluñ cellātu; koli mutaliyaṉa kaṭintāṉeṉṟaluñ cellāteṉṟuṇarntukoḷka. allatūum, aintu kantaṅkaḷiṉ tokiyē uruvāvatallāl, vēṟuruva meṉpatoṉṟilli yeṉpatē numatu kōṭ pāṭākaliṉ, numatu kaṭavuḷukku nīvir urikkum uruvamum at takiyatē yātal vēṇṭum; illātatōr uruvatti avaṉ piṟavikaṭōṟum eṭuppāṉeṉṟalum ataṉāṟ poruntāmiyum kāṇka.
iṉi, num puttamuṉivaṉ vaḻuvillāta piṭakanūlkaḷi aruḷicceytaṉaṉ eṉṟalum ākātu. aṟamum aṟamallātaṉavum viravik kiṭakkum iv vulakattē uyarnta aṟaṅkaḷi ārāyntu piritteṭuttu oru nūlāka aruḷic ceytaṟkup palanāḷum oru toṭarpāka oruvaṟku uṇarvu nikaḻtalvēṇṭum. maṟṟu nīvirō oru kaṇattu nikaḻnta vuṇarvu ataṉ piṟkaṇattu illiyāy māyntoḻiyap, piṟpiṟ kaṇattup putitu putitāka uṇarvu tōṉṟu meṉkiṉṟīr. numatu kūṟṟiṉpaṭi pārkkuṅkāl, numputta muṉivaṉukku oru toṭarpāṉa vuṇarvu illi eṉpatē peṟappaṭumātalāl, toṭarpāṉa vuṇarvu koṇṭaṉṟic ceyyappaṭāta aṟivu nūlkaḷi avaṉ aruḷic ceytāṉeṉṟal numatu kōṭpāṭṭukku muṟṟum muraṇām; atuvēyumaṉṟi, avaṉ an nūlkaḷi aruḷicceyyat toṭaṅkutal eḻuttukkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ accoṟkaḷāl ākkappaṭṭa coṟṟoṭarkaḷeṉṉum karuvikaḷik koṇṭaṉṟip piṟitoruvāṟṟāṉum iyalāmiyiṉālum, oru kaṇattut toṭaṅkiya ōr eḻuttōci ataṉ piṟkaṇattu illiyāy oḻital
vēṇṭu meṉpatē numatu kōḷākaliṉ avvāṟṟāṟ palaveḻuttukkaḷ toṭarnta coṟkaḷum pala coṟkaḷ toṭarnta coṟṟoṭarkaḷum avaṟṟāl ākiya nūlum uḷavātal ākāmiyiṉālum avaṉ an nūlkaḷi ākkiṉāṉ eṉṉum numaturi poruḷil kūṟṟē pōlum!
iṉi, uṭampum uyirum aivakik kantaṅkaḷiṉ tokiyēyallāmal, maṟṟu avi vēṟu taṉip poruḷkaḷ allaveṉṟum, ellāp poruḷkaḷuṅ kaṇaṅkaṭōṟum māyntu māyntu putiyaṉavāyt tōṉṟuvaṉavē yallatu nilipēṟāyuḷḷatu etuvum iṉṟeṉṟuṅ kūṟāniṉṟīr. avvāṟu kaṇaṅkaṭōṟum māṟuvatu ilporuḷāṉa veṟum pāḻō allatu uḷḷatoru poruṭāṉō eṉa viṉāyiṉārkku, aḵtu ilporuḷēyāmeṉa urippirāyiṉ, mayirillāta tiṇṇiya yāmi ōṭṭiṉkaṇ mayirmuḷittateṉṟum, ammayirāṟ kampalam neytu malaṭipeṟṟa makaṉ pōrttuk koṇṭā ṉeṉṟuṅ kūṟum muḻup poyyurikkē, nīr kūṟum illataṉkaṇ ellāntōṉṟik kaṇaṅka ṭōṟum māyum eṉum pōliyuri oppām. aṟṟaṉṟu, uḷḷatoru poruḷē paruvaṭivil māṟutaluṟṟuk keṭṭu maṭiyum, nuṇvaṭivil eṉṟum uḷatām eṉ ṟurikkuvirāyiṉ atu civarākiya ematu kōṭpāṭāvatē yaṉṟip puttarākiya numatu kōṭpā ṭaṉṟu.
aṉṟākavē, paruvaṭiviṟ kaṭpulaṉāy niṟkum ivvuṭampiṟkē māṟutaluṅ kēṭumallāmal itaṟku mutalāy niṟkum nuṇporuḷāṉa māyikku avi yilli yeṉṟuṇarvirāka iṉṉum nīvir urikkumāṟē aivakik kantaṅkaḷiṉ tokiyē uyirāy, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟākat toṭarnta uṇarviṟṟākiya uyireṉpatoṉṟu ilatāyiṉ,  nēṟṟu innūli eḻutat tuvaṅkiṉavaṉ yāṉē, iṉṟitaṉi eḻutukiṉṟavaṉum yāṉē, nāḷi itaṉi eḻutuvōṉum yāṉē eṉa oruvaṟku oru toṭarpāṉa vuṇarvu nikaḻā tākalvēṇṭum. maṟṟu, ivvulakattu nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷellām avaṟṟic ceyyum uyirkaḷukku oru toṭarpāṉa vuṇarvu nikaḻumāṟu paṟṟi yaṉṟip piṟitoruvāṟṟāṉum nikaḻāmi ellārkkuṅ kaṇ kūṭāy aṟiyakkiṭattaliṉ, ivvuṇmiyoṭu tiṟampi aivakik kantaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟāyt, toṭarpupaṭṭa vuṇarvuṭiya uyir eṉpatoṉ ṟilliyeṉṟal ciṟitum aṭātu. mēlum, aivakik kantaṅkaḷiyaṉṟi vēṟu uyir illiyāyiṉ, oruvaṉ viḻittirukkiyilum uṟaṅkukiyilum avaṉ oru paṭittāṉa vuṇarvuṭiyaṉātal vēṇṭum. avvāṟaṉṟi viḻittirukkiyiṟ pāmpik kaṇṭālum añcumavaṉ, uṟaṅkukiyil aḵtu avaṉ mēl ēṟic ceṉṟālum ataṉiyavaṉ uṇarātiruttal eṉṉi? viḻippiṉum uṟakkattiṉum av vivakik kantaṅkaḷum uḷavallō? kaṇaṅ kaṭōṟum uṇarvu keṭṭuk keṭṭut tōṉṟumeṉpatē ematu kōṭpāṭākaliṉ viḻippiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum uṇarvu uṟakkattiṉkaṭ keṭumāṟupaṟṟi emakku āvatōr iḻukkilliyeṉpirēl, viḻippiṉkaṇ nikaḻnta vuṇarvu keṭṭu uṟakkattiṉkaṇ vēṟoru puttuṇarvu tōṉṟu māyiṉaṉṟē atu numatu kōṭpāṭṭiṟku icintatām;
avvāṟaṉṟi viḻippiṉkaṇ ellāvuṇarvu muṭiyaṉāyiruntāṉ oruvaṉ ayarnta uṟakkattiṉkaṇ av vuṇarvu oru ciṟitum nikaḻappeṟātu kaṭṭi pōlavuṅ kaṟpōlavun taṉṉimaṟantu kiṭattal kaṇṭāmākaliṉ, itu num matattiṉ poymiyiyē aṟivippataṉṟi ataṟ kevvāṟṟāṉum utaviceyvataṉṟu. atuvēyumaṉṟi, nēṟṟup pakal viḻittiruntak kāl oruvaṟkuṇṭāṉa uṇarvu nēṟṟiravu uṟaṅkac ceṉṟakkāṟ keṭṭu, iṟṟināṭkāliyiṟ putitu tōṉṟumeṉṟalē ematu kōṭṭipāṭṭiṟku ottatām eṉpirēl, nēṟṟup pakal ōr āṭiyi neytaṟkut tuvaṅkiṉāṉ oru kikkōḷaṉ aḵtu aṉṟē muṭintiṭāmiyiṉ, ataṉi arikuṟiyāy viṭṭuvittu, nēṟṟiravu tuyilacceṉṟa aḷavāṉē ataṉi neytaṟkuriya ellā vuṇarvum muṟṟuṅ keṭappeṟṟu, iṟṟik kāliyil attuyil nīṅki eḻuntavaḷavāṉē taṉṉi iṉṉoṉṟu uṇarum uṇarvum nēṟṟut tāṉ neytaṟku eṭuttu arikuṟiyāy viṭṭuvitta āṭiyip paṟṟiya vuṇarvum ākiya ellām muṟṟum iḻantu, iṟṟikkut taṉṉi oru kuyavaṉāka niṉintu kuṭamum pāṉiyum vaṉiyap pukutalvēṇṭum. nēṟṟut taṉṉi aracaṉāyk karutiṉāṉoruvaṉ iṟṟikkut taṉṉi ōr ēvaṟkāraṉākavum, iṟṟikkuk kūliyāḷāyiruppāṉ nāḷikku aracaṉākavum iṉṟikku makkaṭ piṟaviyiruppatu nāḷikkut taṉṉi oru vilaṅkup piṟaviyākavum, nēṟṟup peṇṇāyiruntatu iṉṟu āṇākavum, iṉṟu āṇā yiruppatu nāḷip peṇṇākavum nēṟṟuk kallāyiruntatu iṉṟu taṉṉi oru maramākavum, iṉṟu maramā yiruppatu nāḷit taṉṉi oru makaṉākavuṅ karutik koḷḷal vēṇṭum!
tiruvaruṭ ceyalāl attakiya poym māṟṟavuṇarvum, atupaṟṟi varakkaṭavaṉāya peruṅ kuḻappaṅkaḷum ilavāy, aṟivillāp poruḷ mutaṟkoṇṭu ōraṟivu īraṟivu mūvaṟivu nālaṟivu aiyaṟivu āṟaṟivu uṭiya uyirkaḷ īṟāka ellām orumuṟiyuḷ aṭaṅkit toṭarpupaṭṭa niliyum uṇarvum vāyntaṉavāy mika viyattakka oḻuṅkōṭum naṭipeṟak kāṇṭal kaṟṟār mutaṟ kallātār īṟāṉa ettiṟattārkkum oppa muṭintatākaliṟ, ciṟitu kaṟṟu ārāycciyuṇarvu ilarāyiṉāri mayakkum num pōlik koḷki evarkkum ettakiya payaṉun taruvataṉṟeṉa uṇarvirāka. aṟṟēl, nēṟṟu nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷiṟ cilavum, muntiya tiṅkaḷil nikaḻntavikaḷ palavum, muṉṉāṇṭukaḷil nikaḻntavaṟṟil mikaccila tavira ēṉiyellāmum muṟpiṟavikaḷil nikaḻntavaṟṟiṟ cilatāmumiṉṟi muṟṟum maṟantoḻiya vēṟuvēṟuṇurvukaḷ putiya putiyavāy nammaṉōrmāṭṭellāṅ kaṇkūṭāyk kāṇap paṭutaliṉ, ematu koḷki kuṟṟamātal yāṅṅaṉameṉṟu ucāvutirāyiṉ, muṉ nāḷiṉum muṟṟiṅkaḷiṉum, muṉṉāṇṭukaḷiṉum, muṟ piṟavikaḷiṉum nikaḻnta uṇarvu nikaḻccikaḷ māyntu pōyiṉa veṉṟal uṇmiyaṉṟu; avvuṇarvu nikaḻccikaḷ attuṇiyum oru tiṉiyaḷavuṅ keṭāmal makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉum ēṉiyuyirkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkaḷiṉum naṉku patintēyirukkiṉṟaṉa. allākkāl, oru cilar vēṟu palariṉum mikka niṉivuṭiyarāyk kaḻintakāla nikaḻccikaḷi yellām naṉku niṉivukūrtal ākātu.
cilar niṉiviṉ mikkavarāyum palar atiṟ kuṟintavarāyuṅ kāṇappaṭutaṟku ētu veṉṉiyeṉṟu nuṇuki nōkkumvaḻi, niṉi viṉāṟṟal mikuntuḷḷārkku avaruḷḷattip potinta aṟiyāmi
yiruḷ vilakiniṉṟu avartam aṟivu cuṭarntu viḷaṅka iṭantan tirukkiṉṟa teṉṟum, niṉiviṉāṟṟal mikak kuṟin tuḷḷārkku avaratu uḷḷattik kavinta aṟiyāmiyiruḷ vilaki nillāmiyiṉ avarataṟivu avvāṟu viḷaṅkutaṟku iṭam ilatāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum av vēṟupāṭṭiṉ uṇmi pulappaṭāniṟkum. iv vēṟupāṭṭiṉi ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉ kaṇ vittuk kāṭṭutum: īram ēṟiya viṟakil tī uḷatāyiṉum, av īram uḷḷaḷavun ataṉkaṇ at tī pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟātākum; maṟṟu avvīram nīṅkiyavaḻi avviṟakiṉkaṇ atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. iṅṅaṉamē, aṟiyāmi yuḷḷaḷavum uyiriṉ aṟiviṟ patinta uṇarvu nikaḻccikaḷ pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟāvākum; aṟiyāmi nīṅkanīṅka ataṉkaṭ patinta nikaḻccikaḷellām paṭippaṭiyāyp pulappaṭṭu niṉiviṟku varum. kalvikkaḻakam oṉṟiṟ payilum māṇākkar pallōrum orēvakiyāṉa niṉivuṭiyarāy illāmiyum, avaruḷ aṟivu mikutiyum uṭiyār cilarkku niṉivu mikkut tōṉṟu talum, mutalil aṟiyāmiyāṟ kavarappaṭṭiruntārum payiluntōṟum payiluntōṟum avvaṟiyāmi ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tēya ammuṟiyē avaratu  niṉivum paṭippaṭiyāy valivuṟṟu viḷaṅkit tāṅkaṟṟaporuḷkaḷi maṟavātu niṉivu kūrtalum ematu civakkoḷkiyiṉ meymmiyi naṉku nilipeṟuttum. atuvēyumaṉṟit, tavattāṉ maṉan tūyarāy aṟiyāmi tēyappeṟṟa aṭiyār cilar muṉṉum muṟpiṟavikaḷiṉum nikaḻntavaṟṟi yellām oruṅkuṇartali nūlkaḷāṉum, kaṇkūṭāṉum iṭi yiṭiyē aṟitumākaliṉ muṉnikaḻnta uṇarvu nikaḻcci kaḷellām muṟṟum māyntupōkap, piṉ puttuṇarccikaḷ uṇṭāmeṉṉum numaturi uṇmi yaṉṟātal teḷiyak kaṭavirāka. iṉi, makkaḷallāta ciṟṟuyirkaḷum toṭarpupaṭṭa vuṇarvuṭimiyiṉālētāṉ tattam vāḻnāḷ elli yaḷavum uyirvāḻkiṉṟaṉa.
paṟavikaḷ tām muṭṭiyiṭuṅ kālam vantatuṇi yāṉē palanāṭkaḷum muyaṉṟu kūṭukaḷ kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak kūṭukaḷik kaṭṭattuvaṅkiya nāḷmutal avi muṭivu peṟuṅkāṟum ap paṟavikaḷ toṭarnta vuṇarviṉavāy avitammic ceytu muṭittal kaṇṭāmaṉṟē. avi tamakkut toṭarnta vuṇarvu illiyāyiṉ, muṉ nāṭkaḷiṟ kaṭṭattuvaṅkiya kūṭukaḷi maṟantu piṉ nāṭkaḷil avi vēṟu palavaṟṟic ceyyat tuvaṅkit tuvaṅki oṉṟiyuñ ceytumuṭikka māṭṭāvāy oḻital vēṇṭum; tām muṉnāḷil īṉṟa kuñcukaḷiyum maṟantu avaṟṟiṟku iri tarātoḻitalum vēṇṭum. ivvāṟellām numatu koḷkippaṭi oru ciṟitāyiṉum naṭipeṟak kāṇāmi yāṉum, ulakattiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum ellā nikaḻccikaḷum ematu civakkoḷkiyiṉpaṭiyē oru toṭarpāy ōr oḻuṅkiṉkaṭ paṭṭu naṭipeṟak kāṇṭalāṉum oru toṭarpāṉa vuṇarvuṭiya uyir illiyeṉum numatu kōḷ ulaka vaḻakkōṭum āṉṟōr vaḻakkōṭum ovvāta poyk kōḷāmeṉṟu kaṭippiṭippīrāka.
iṉi, itukāṟum yām kūṟiyatukoṇṭē aintu kantaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟāyt toṭarnta vuṇarvuṭiya uyirkaḷ pala uḷaveṉpatum, toṭarnta vuṇarvuṭiya avvuyir kaḷiṉ uḷḷattit toṉṟutoṭṭē aṟiyāmi yeṉṉum ōriruḷ maṟittukkoṇṭiruttaliṉ avaṟṟi ṉuṇarvukaḷ muṟṟum viḷaṅkāmal iṭiyiṭiyē maṟintu pōkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatum, avvaṟiyāmi nīkkattiṉ poruṭ ṭākavē iv vuṭampum iv vuṭampiṉkaṇ aminta viyattakum uṟuppukaḷum, ivvulakamum, ivvulakattup pal poruḷ kaḷum ellāmvalla civaperumāṉāl ivvuyirkaṭku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum, ikkaruvikaḷiṉ utavikoṇṭu tamatu aṟiyāmiyit tolittu uṇarvu muḻutum viḷaṅkic civapirāṉ ṟiruvaṭip pēriṉpattip peṟutalē uyirkaṭku vīṭupēṟāmallatu uṇarvu muḻutuṅkeṭṭu mīṇṭum periyatōr aṟiyāmiyiṟ ceṉṟu pukuvatākiya niruvāṇamē vīṭupēṟāmeṉak kūṟum numatu kōṭpāṭu peritum piḻipaṭuvatā meṉpatum tāmē peṟappaṭutal kāṇka eṉṟu aṭikaḷ aruḷicceytār.
atukiṭakka uṭampum ulakamum ākiya karuvikaḷi uyiriṉkaṭ kūṭṭutaṟkuc civam eṉṟu oru kaṭavuḷ vēṇṭiṟṟeṉṉi? oruvaṉ uṇarvōṭu ceyta ceyalāl viṉi yeṉpatoṉṟu uṇṭāka, avaṉum avvuṇarvum aḻintupaṭṭa viṭattum avviṉi aḻiyātāy niṉṟu avaṉukkup piṉṉum piṉṉum piṟavikaḷi uṇṭākkum eṉṟalē ellārkkum oppamuṭintatām, eṉṟu apputta muṉivaṉ iṭimaṟuttuk kūṟappiṉṉum avaṉuriyi maṟuttu aṭikaḷ aruḷic ceytār:
piṟavi uṇṭātaṟku viṉi ōr ētuvāmeṉpatē ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭāvataṉṟi, av viṉiyē piṟaviyi uṇṭākku meṉṟal evarkkum ottataṉṟu. kūlikku ūḻiyañ ceytāṉ oruvaṉukku, avaṉ ceyta ūḻiyamē avaṉukkuk kūliyik koṭātu; avaṉ ceyta ūḻiyattiṉ iyalpaṟintu ataṉi avaṟkut taruvāṉāṉa talivaṉ oruvaṉ iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum.
aḵtu etaṉāleṉiṉ, avaṉ ceyta ūḻiyamākiya viṉi aṟivuṭiyatumaṉṟu, avaṟkuriya kūliyiṉit taravallatumaṉṟu, atupōla oru piṟaviyil oruvaṉ āṟṟiya viṉiyiṉ tokutiyum aṟivilatākaliṉ, avaṟku iṉit tarattakka piṟavi iṉṉateṉa aṟiyavum māṭṭātu, ataṉi atu tāṉē paṭittuk koṭukkavum vallataṉṟu, iṉi muṟṟuṇarvum atupaṟṟi nikaḻum pērāṟṟalum ciṟṟaṟiviṉarāy uyirkaṭku iṉmi teḷiyakkiṭanta toṉṟākaliṉ, avar tāmē tamakku vēṇṭum piṟaviyi amittukkoḷḷa māṭṭuvārum allar;
iṅṅaṉam viṉi tāṉē piṟaviyit tarātāka, viṉi ceytārum atu taṉṉip paṭittukkoḷḷārāka, avvavar viṉikkut takka piṟaviyit tarutaṟku ellāvaṟivum ellā āṟṟalum mutaṉmiyum oruṅku uṭiya iṟivaṉ oruvaṉ iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭu meṉka. mēlum, ūḻiyañ ceytāṉ oruvaṉ iruntē kūliyip peṟutal vēṇṭumākaliṉ, viṉiceyta uyir aḻiyātiruntē ataṉ payaṉāṉa piṟaviyip peṟutal vēṇṭum; ataṉālum uyir illiyāka viṉivāṟṟāṉum ovvāteṉ ṟuṇarka.
aṟṟēl, nīr kūṟum av viṟivaṟkuriya ilakkaṇantāṉ eṉṉi? eṉṟu ap putta muṉivaṉ viṉāva, aṭikaḷ aruḷic ceyvār.

11. civa camayak
kōṭpāṭukaḷi viḷakkal
emmiṟivaṉ eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaṭku uṭampukaḷit tantu, avi tammi eṇṇiṟanta ulakaṅkaḷil vittu,. avi tammi iyaṟkiyē potinta aṟiyāmiyi nīkki avitamakku aṟivuccuṭar koḷuttal vēṇṭum pērirakkamum aruḷum uṭiyaṉākaliṉ, avaṉ avvuyirkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum avvulakaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum eḷḷum eṇṇeyumpōṟ kalantu niṟpaṉāvaṉ.
 naḷḷuṅ kīḻuḷum mēluḷum yāvuḷum
  eḷḷum eṇṇeyum pōlniṉṟa entiyē.						(tiruccatakam,46)		
iṅṅaṉam, avvuyirkaḷum ulakaṅkaḷum vēṟu, tāṉ vēṟu, eṉṟu pirittukkāṇal iyalāvāṟu, avi tammōṭu oruṅkiyintu aviyē tāṉāy iraṇṭaṟṟu niṟku niliyum, aṅṅaṉam niṟpiṉum ciṟṟaṟiviṉavākiya uyirkaḷiṉum aṟivillaṉavāy māṟun taṉmiyavākiya ulakaṅkaḷiṉum vēṟāntaṉmi yuṭimiyiṉ avaṟṟiṉ vēṟāy oru peṟṟiyaṉāy niṟkum uṇmi niliyum uṭiyaṉām.
iruḷi veḷiyē ikapara māki iruntavaṉē.						(nīttal viṇṇappam,17)
 īcaṉēnī yalla tilli iṅkum aṅkum eṉpatum
  pēci ṉēṉōr pēta miṉmi.	(tiruccatakam, 78)
 peṇṇāki āṇāy aliyāyp piṟaṅkuoḷicēr
  viṇṇāki ittaṉiyum vēṟāki; 	(tiruvempāvi, 18)
 pōṟṟiip puvaṉa nīrtīk
	kāloṭu vāṉam āṉāy
  pōṟṟiev vuyirkkum tōṟṟa
	mākinī tōṟṟa millāy
  pōṟṟiel lāvu yirkkum
	īṟāyī ṟiṉmiyāṉāy
  pōṟṟiaim pulaṉkaḷ niṉṉip
	puṇarkilāp puṇarkki yaṉē.	(tiruccatakam,70)
 pittaṉē ellā vuyirumāyt taḻittup
  piḻittu avi alliyāy niṟkum.
  ettaṉē		(piṭittapattu, 8)
 vāṉkeṭṭu mārutam māyntu aḻalnīr
	maṇkeṭiṉun
  tāṉkeṭṭa liṉṟic calippu aṟiyāt
	taṉmiyaṉukku.	(tiruttēḷḷēṉam, 18)
ulakattuk kāṇappaṭum uyirkaḷiṉ uṭampu kaḷellām āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irukūṟṟiṉkaṭpaṭutaliṉ, av viruvaki yuṭampukaḷiṉ niṟkum uyirkaḷum avvuṭampu kaḷōṭotta āṇṭaṉmiyum peṇṭaṉmiyum uṭiyaṉavēyām eṉpatu peṟutum. peṟavē, ivvuyir kaṭkellām mutal uyirāy niṟkuṅ kaṭavuḷum āṇum peṇṇumākiya īruyirāy ammiyapparāy niṟkumeṉa uṇarntu ivvāṟiruvakippaṭṭu niṟkum iṟimutaṟ poruḷkaḷuḷ, iṟivaṉ taṉ iṟiviyōṭu pirippiṉṟi uṭaṅkiyintu niṟka, iṟivi ulakuyirkaḷil avvāṟu oruṅkiyintu niṟka iṅṅaṉamē ellāp poruḷkaḷum uyirkaḷum ammuḻumutaṟ poruḷōṭu kūṭiniṉṟu āṇum peṇṇumāyt toḻiṟpaṭā niṟkiṉṟaṉa veṉ ṟaṟivirāka. iṅṅaṉa mallākkāl, āṇum peṇṇumāy niṉṟu naṭipeṟum ivvulakattiṉ iyalpu vēṟu evvāṟṟāṉum viḷaṅkā teṉṟaṟika.
 uṭiyāḷ uṉṟaṉ naṭuvirukkum
	uṭiyāḷ naṭuvuḷ nīyirutti
  aṭiyēṉ naṭuvuḷ iruvīrum iruppatāṉāl,				(kōyiṉmūtta tiruppatikam, 1)
pantaṇi viraliyum nīyumniṉ aṭiyār
	paḻaṅkuṭil toṟumeḻun taruḷiya paraṉē.				(tiruppaḷḷiyeḻucci, 8)
aṇṇal taṉ orupāl
	avaḷ attaṉām makaṉām.	(tirucciṟṟampalakkōviyār,112)
yāviyum ām ēkattu oruvaṉ.	(tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyār, 71)
iṉi, ulakaṅkaḷilum uyirkaḷilum maṟintu niṉṟu avi tammi iyaṅkum iṟivaṉ atupaṟṟi aruvamāy niṟpaṉākaliṉ, avaṉi aṉparāyiṉāraṉṟi vēṟevarum uṇaravuṅ kāṇavum vallunarallar; aṅṅaṉam aṉparal lārāl aṟiyappaṭāṉāyiṉum, aṉparāyiṉarkkut taṉatu aruḷālāya uruvattiru mēṉiyiṟ ṟōṉṟip pēraruḷ puritalum uṭiyaṉ. eṉavē, avaṟku aruvam uruvam eṉṉum iruvakit tirumēṉiyum uḷavām. avaṉ taṉatu aruḷāl mēṟkoḷḷum uruvattirumēṉi, nammaṉōr viṉivaḻiyē oru tāyiṉ karuppiyuṭ pukunteṭukkum ūṉuṭampiṉ uruppōlvataṉṟu; aḵtavaṉ niṉintavaḷavāṉē aruḷiṟ ṟōṉṟi aruḷil maṟivatākum; ataṉāl, avaṟku eññāṉṟun tāy tantiyar ilareṉat teḷimiṉ!
yāvarāyiṉum aṉparaṉṟi
	aṟiyoṇāmalarc cōtiyāṉ.	(ceṉṉippattu,1)
mūvarum muppattu mūvarum maṟṟoḻinta
	tēvaruṅ kāṇāc civaperumāṉ.	(tiruveṇpā.9)
pūvēṟu kōṉum purantaraṉum poṟpaminta
  nāvēṟu celviyum nāraṇaṉum nāṉmaṟiyum
  māvēṟu cōtiyum vāṉavarun tāmaṟiyāc
 cēvēṟu cēvaṭi	(tirukkōttumpi,1)
āvā ariayaṉ intiraṉ
	vāṉōrkku ariya civaṉ
  vāvā eṉṟu eṉṉiyum pūtalattē
	valit tāṇṭu koṇṭāṉ.	(tirutteḷḷēṇam, 7)
patti valiyiṟ paṭuvōṉ kāṇka.	(tiruvaṇṭappakuti, 42)
aruvāy uruvamum āṉapirāṉ.	(tirutteḷḷēṇam, 2)
urumūṉṟu māki uṇarvaritām oruvaṉumē.	(tiruppūvalli, 6)
centaḻalpuri tirumēṉiyuṅ kāṭṭi.	(tiruppaḷḷi yeḻucci, 8)
toṇṭaṉēṟku uḷḷavā vantu tōṉṟiṉāy
  kaṇṭuṅ kaṇṭilēṉ eṉṉakaṇ māyamē.	(tiruccatakam, 42)
maṟṟum yāvarkkun
  tantiyāy tampirāṉ taṉakku aḵtu ilāṉ.	(tiruccatakam, 47)
iṉi, aṉparāyiṉārkku aruḷceytaṟ poruṭṭu emmiṟivaṉ eṭukkun tiruvuruvam tīyiṉōṭu oppatākum; ataṉāl, aṉaṟ piḻampiyē em aiyaṉukku oru tirumēṉiyākavum aṟivunūlkaḷ kūṟāniṟkum. aṟṟēl, ulakiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum viṭuttut, tīyi iṟivaṟkoru tirumēṉiyākak kūṟuvatu eṉṉi yeṉpirēl, maṇ puṉal aṉal kāl veḷi eṉṉum aimperum pakuppilum, uyirilum ellāp poruḷkaḷum aṭaṅkum; ivitammuṭ kāṟṟum veḷiyum uyirum kaṭpulaṉākātavi yākaliṉ, avi uruvattirumēṉikku ēṟṟaṉa ākā; eñciya maṇ puṉal aṉal eṉpavaṟṟil maṇṇum nīruṅ kiyāṟ paṟṟappaṭum parup poruḷēyāka iṟivaṉ mēṟkoḷḷum aruḷuruvō aṅṅaṉaṅ kiyāṟ paṟṟappaṭum parupporuḷ aṉṟākalāṉum, aviyiraṇṭun tōṉṟiyapaṭiyē kiṭappaṉavallāl iṟivaṉuruppōl tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ maṟiyun taṉmiya allavākalāṉum avviraṇṭum iṟivaṉuruvat tirumēṉiyōṭu oppaṉavalla. maṟṟu, aṉaṟ koḻuntō kiyāṟ paṟṟap paṭāmiyāṉum, kiyāṟ paṟṟappaṭā tāyiṉuṅ kaṭpulaṉukku viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum uruvuṭimiyāṉum, uṭaṉtōṉṟi uṭaṉ maṟiyun taṉmit tākalāṉum aḵtoṉṟumē iṟivaṉatu uruvattirumēṉiyōṭu oppatūum ataṟku iṭamāvatūum ām.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, iṟivaṉ iyaṟkiyoḷiyuṭiyaṉāy uyirkaḷiṉ akaviruḷākiya aṟiyāmiyi nīkki aṟivoḷi tōṟṟuvittalpōlat, tīkkoḻuntum iyaṟki yoḷiyuṭittāyk kaṇṇi maṟikkum puṟaviruḷiyōṭṭi evaṟṟiyum viḷaṅkac ceytalāṉum, iṟivaṉ paṭittal kāttal aḻittal aruḷal eṉṉun toḻilkaḷic ceytalpōlat, tīyum ōrelliyuḷ niṉṟu uyirōṭukūṭiya uṭampukaḷit tōṟṟi nilipeṟuvittut taṉṉelli kaṭantavaḻi avaṟṟi yaḻittu uyirkaṭku aṟivuviḷaṅkac ceytalāṉum, kaṭpulaṉāyt tōṉṟum iṟivaṉ uṭaṉē maṟintu eṅkum aruvamāy niṟṟal pōlak kaṭpulaṉāyt tōṉṟun tīyum maṟintavaḻi eṅkum uḷatākalāṉum, iṟivaṉ tāṉ tūyaṉāy niṉṟē tūyavallāp poruḷkaḷōṭu viraviya vaḻiyum tāṉ avaṟṟāṟ paṟṟappaṭāṉāy avaṟṟiṉ tūvāmi nīkkit tūymi ceytalpōla, tīyum tāṉ tūyatāy niṉṟē tūyavallāp poruḷkaḷōṭu kūṭiyak kālum tāṉ avaṟṟāl aḻukkaṭiyātu avaṟṟiṉ aḻukkiyellām erittut tūya cāmparākkutalāṉum, aruvum uruvum oḷiyum oruṅkuṭiya iṟivaṉip pōlavē aṉaṟ koḻuntum aruvum uruvum oḷiyum oruṅkuṭiyatāy niṟṟalāṉum kaṭpuṉalāyttōṉṟun tīyē kaṭpulaṉākā iṟivaṉatu aruḷuruvat tirumēṉi viḷakkattiṟku iṭamāmeṉṟu teḷiyaṟpāṟṟu. itu paṟṟiyaṉṟē, ulakattiṉkaṇ āṇṭāṇṭirunta paṇṭimakkaḷ ellārum tīvaṭiviṉum, tīyiṉ kūṟāṉa ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ vaṭiviṉum vittu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu varalāyiṉā reṉka. iṉṉum āṇṭaṉmikku aṭiyāḷamāṉa kaṭuntaṉmiyuṭiya tī civanta niṟam uṭiyatātal paṟṟiyē āṇ uruviṉaṉākiya civapirāṉukkuc civanta tirumēṉiyum. at tīyiṉkaṇ aṭaṅkit tōṉṟuṅ kuḷirnta nīrvaṭivu nīlaniṟa muṭiyatātal paṟṟiyē iṟivaṉākiya civapirāṉkaṇ aṭaṅki miḷirum melliya aruṭperum peṇṭaṉmiyuṭiya iṟivikku nīlaniṟat tirumēṉiyum nūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭuvavāyiṉa: ulakattuḷḷa ellā uyirkaḷum, poruḷkaḷum, poruṭṭaṉmikaḷum, niṟaṅkaḷum, āṇ, peṇ, tī, nīr, veppan taṭpam, cemmi nīlam eṉa iru perum vakuppil aṭaṅkum iyalpiṉi nuṉittārāyuṅkāl, ivi yellāvaṟṟiṟkum mutalāy niṟkum iṟivaṉum iṟiviyum avviru vakiyiyalpu muṭiyarām uṇmi tāṉē viḷaṅkāniṟkum. iṉit, tīyiṉkaṇ iṟivaṉ muṉittu viḷaṅkutal paṟṟiyē at tīppiḻampiṉ vaṭivākat tiraṇṭu nīṇṭu kuvinta civaliṅkat tiruvaṭivam tirukkōyil kaṭōṟum niṟutti vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa teṉavum, civamākiya neruppiṟ paṭinta aṭiyārkku avarip paṟṟiya mummalaṅkaḷum ataṉāl erikkappaṭṭut tūyavāy avari varuttātoḻiya avarum muṟṟun tūyarākic civapirāṉ tiruvaruḷiṟ ṟōyntu pēriṉpam nukarntiruttalit terikkum aṭiyāḷamākavē āviṉ cāṇatti neruppiliṭṭuc cāmparākki mūṉṟu variyāka neṟṟimēliṭṭum meyyeṅkum pūciyum veḷḷiya tūya āṉēṟṟiṉi ac civaliṅkavaṭiviṉ muṉvittum āṉṟōrellām paṇṭutoṭṭut tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭu āṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉa reṉavum uṇarntukoḷka.

  tīyēyeṉa maṉṉuciṟṟam palavar.				(tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyār, 370)
centaḻalpuri tirumēṉiyuṅkāṭṭi;	(tiruppaḷḷi yeḻucci, 8)

arattamēṉiyāy.	(tiruccatakam, 93)
piṅkuvaḷik kārmalarāṟ ceṅkamalap pimpōtāl
 aṅkaṅ kurukiṉattāṟ piṉṉum aravattāl

 poṅku maṭuvil.	(tiruvempāvi, 13)

kōmāṉ niṉ tirukkōyil
  tūkēṉ meḻukēṉ.	(tiruccatakam, 14)
iṉi aṟiyāmiyiṉum, aṟiyāmi vāyilāka varum iruviṉiyiṉum, ivi iraṇṭaṉ nīkkattiṉ poruṭṭu iṟivaṉāl ulakum uṭampumākat tarappaṭṭa māyiyiṉuṅ kiṭantu uḻalum uyirkaḷ ōraṟivuṭiya pul mutal āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷ tēvar īṟākap palappala piṟavikaḷ eṭuttu, avaṟṟāl aṟiyāmi tēyntu tēyntu aṟivu viḷaṅka viḷaṅka, iṟutiyil iṟivaṉē kuruvaṭiviṟ ṟōṉṟi avarkku muṟṟaṟivu viḷaṅkac ceytu, avarit taṉatu tiruvaruṭ pēriṉ
pattil aṭakkik koḷvaṉ. ituvē vīṭupēṟām; ippēṟṟi eytiṉārkku mīḷap piṟavi uṇṭākātu, eññāṉṟum oru peṟṟittām aḻiyāp pēriṉpamē uḷatām. ippeṟṟittām pēriṉpam iṟivaṉmāṭ ṭaṉṟi vēṟeṅkum iṉmiyāṉum, varampilaṟivu varampilāṟṟal mutalāka iṟivaṟkuriya ēṉi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟiṉum varampi liṉpamuṭimi yākiya ītoṉṟumē iṟivaṟkuc ciṟappilakkaṇa mākalāṉum, tuṉpattiṟ kiṭantuḻalum uyirkaḷ ēṉi niṉivukaḷi yellām viṭṭu viṭṭu avaṟkuriya av viṉpavuruviṉi iṭiyaṟātu niṉivu kūrakkūra avvuyirkaḷ avaṉṟaṉ iṉpavuruviṉi eytumākalāṉum, av viṉpavuruviṉi av vuyirkaṭku niṉivil ēṟṟutaṟkaṇ iṟivaṟkuriya ēṉi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi yuṇarttum ēṉip peyarkaḷellā vaṟṟilum aṉpu iṉpam eṉap poruṭantu avaṉṟaṉ iṉpa vuruviṉi maṭṭum niṉivuṟuttum civam eṉṉuñ collē kaḻiperuñ ciṟappiṟṟāy niliyutalāṉum ellāmvalla iṟivaṟkuc civam eṉṉum appeyarē peyarāka em muṉṉōrāṟ ṟoṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varalāyiṟṟeṉ ṟuṇarntu koḷka.
valviṉiyēṉ ṟaṉṉi, maṟintiṭa mūṭiya māyiruḷi.				(civapurāṇam, 50, 51)
kaḻivil karuṇiyik kāṭṭik kaṭiyaviṉi yakaṟṟip
  paḻamalam paṟṟaṟut tāṇṭavaṉ.	(tiruppāṇṭip patikam, 8)

	kāyappiṟappōṭu iṟappeṉṉum
  aṟampāva meṉṟiraṇ ṭaccan
	tavirtteṉṉi yāṇṭukoṇṭāṉ,	(tirutteḷḷēṇam, 8)
pullākip pūṭāyp puḻuvāy maramākip
 palviruka mākip paṟaviyāyp pāmpākik

  tēṟaliṉ teḷivē civaperu māṉē

  īṟilāp pataṅkaḷ yāviyuṅ kaṭanta
	iṉpamē eṉṉuṭi aṉpē.	(kōyiṟṟiruppatikam, 1)
iṉi, yām vaḻipaṭum iṉpavuruviṟṟāṉa civam muḻu mutaṟkaṭavuḷē yaṉṟip piṟitu aṉṟu eṉpataṟkup piṟavāmi, iṟavāmi, eṉṟum oru peṟṟittāy niṟkum pēriṉpanili, mutalāka em muṉṉōr paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum kaṭavuḷilakkaṇaṅkaḷ em miṟivaṉākiya civapirāṉmāṭṭu eññāṉṟum piṟaḻā iyalpiṉavāyk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉ purāṇaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum nāṉmukaṉ māl kālavuruttiraṉ eṉṉum mūvaril oruvaṉ allaṉ; āriya vētaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mittiraṉ cīkaṇṭavuruttiraṉ mutalāṉa tēvarkaḷuḷḷum oruvaṉ allaṉ; ittēvarka ḷellārum piṟappum iṟappum tuṉpamum uṭiyareṉa annūlkaḷilēyē collappaṭutaliṉ avaruḷ evarum muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷākiya civam ākār. mummala vayappaṭṭa ciṟṟayirkaḷākiya avarellān tavattāl emmiṟivaṉaruḷip peṟṟut tēvarāyiṉar. ataṉāl, em muṉṉōrum yāmum emmaṉōrum accivamākiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaṉit teyvamākak kaṉavilum niṉiyēm. iṉṉum, iv viṉpavuru viṟṟākiya civam ellāvuyirkaṭkun taṉatu pēriṉpatti ūṭṭal vēṇṭiyum, talivaṉākiya taṉṉiyum uyirkaḷākiya tam miyalpiyum uṇarāmaṟ ciṟṟuyirkaḷellām aṟiyāmiyāṟ kavarappaṭ ṭiruttaliṉ avi tamakku avvaṟiyāmiyi nīkki aṟivuc cuṭar koḷuttal vēṇṭiyum piṟavikaṭōṟum avaṟṟiṟku mika viyattakka amippōṭu kūṭiya palavē ṟuṭampukaḷi amittuk koṭuttu varukiṉṟāṉ. ovvōr uyirum taṉ ṟaṉakku variyaṟukkappaṭṭa kālam variyil taṉṟaṉ uṭampi liruntu aṟivu viḷaṅkal vēṇṭumākaliṉ, avvuyirkaḷiṉ uṭampi uṇaviṉporuṭṭuc citittalum citippittalum avaṉ karuttukku muṟṟum māṟāy, irakka millāk koṭun tīviṉi yākum. ākavē, ellā vuyirkaḷum tattam uṭampukaḷil iruntu uyirvāḻtaṟku ēṟṟa utavikaḷi emmāliyaṉṟa variyiṟ ceyyak kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. evvuyiriyuṅ kollāmiyum, koṉṟu ataṉ ūṉit tiṉṉāmiyum ākiya aruḷoḻukkam, evvuyirkkum iṉpattic ceyyum civapirāṉi vaḻipaṭuñ civarākiya emakkē uriya ciṟanta aṟamāmeṉat tēṟumiṉ! iṉi uyir eṉpatuṅ kaṭavuḷ eṉpatum illiyeṉṉum numatu koḷkiyum, evvuyiriyuṅ kollal ākātu eṉṉum numatu aṟivuriyum, āyiṉum piṟarāṟ koṉṟu camikkap paṭṭa ōr uyiriṉ ūṉit tiṉṉum numatu ceykiyum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu māṟupaṭutaliṉ, aruḷoḻukkattiṟku muraṇāṉa numatu puttacamayam muṟṟum poyyāmeṉṟu teḷintukoḷḷak kaṭavīrāka!

	taṉakku aḵtu ilāṉ	(tiruccatakam, 47)

uvalic camayaṅkaḷ ovvāta cāttiramām
 cavalikkaṭal	(tirutteḷḷēṇam, 17)
puttaṉ mutalāya pullaṟiviṟ palcamayam	(tiruttōṇōkkam, 6)
iṉi uṭampōṭu kūṭi yiyaṅkum ellā uyirkaḷum viḻit tirukkiyilum uṟaṅkukiyilum avi tam uṭampuḷ ḷiruntu ōvātu acivatu neñcappi oṉṟumēyākum. ivvacivu avvuṭampukaḷiliruntu palavaki muyaṟcikaḷip puriyum uyirkaḷ tammāl naṭattappaṭuvataṉṟu. tammuṭampukkuḷ ḷirukkum neñcappiyiṉ amivum, aḵtu vṅṅaṉameṭipeṟukiṉṟabtaṉamtaṉVtuvumcṇaramāṭṭātacyirkaḷmtaṉimṅṅaṉamXvātumcikkavallavātalvvvāṟuikakūṭum?an neñcappi aciyuṅkāṟum uyirkaḷ uṭampukaḷ nilipeṟutalum, ataṉ acivu niṉṟa
vuṭaṉē uyir uṭampi viṭṭu nīṅkutaluṅ kaṇkūṭāy aṟiyakkiṭanta uṇmikaḷām. ivvāṟu uyirkaḷ nilipeṟutaṟkum nīṅkutaṟkum mutal ētuvāy naṭipeṟum neñcappiyiṉ acivu an neñcappiyit tamakku urimiyāvuṭiya uyirkaṭkē oru ciṟitum viḷaṅkāmi yāṉum, viḷaṅkātatōṭu ataṉi acikkum āṟṟalum avi tamakku illāmiyāṉum, ellā āṟṟalum uṭiya ōr aṟivupporuḷ acittālaṉṟi avvacivu naṭipeṟutal oruvāṟṟāṉum ākāmiyāṉum, ellāmvalla iṟivaṉē an neñcappikaṭōṟum muṉittu viḷaṅkiniṉṟu avi tammi iyakki, avvāṟṟāl uyirkaḷ uṭampukaḷil nilipeṟaniṉṟu aṟivuntoḻilum uṭiyarāy pōtara utavi ceytu varukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu teḷimiṉ! iṅṅaṉam ellā vuyirkaḷiṉ akattē neñcattāmariyiṉkaṭ civapirāṉ muṉittuniṉṟu āṭi ataṉi āṭṭuvikkum aruḷ nikaḻcciyi niṉivukūrntu makkaḷellārum ataṉkaṭ ṭamatu uṇarviṉi yoṭukki vaḻipaṭutaṟku eḷitām poruṭṭē, puṟattē in nilamaṭantikku neñcattāmari pōl vayaṅkuvatākiya it tilli ampalattē av vāṇṭavaṉ aruṭkūttiṉ nili emmuṉṉōrāl amittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa teṉṟuṇarka. akattē uṭampilum puṟattē ulakat tilum emmiyaṉ iṅṅaṉan tirukkūttu nikaḻtti avi tammi iyakkutal paṟṟiyē avaṉ ampalak kūttaṉ eṉavum peyar peṟuvaṉ eṉka. iṅṅaṉam emmiṟivaṉ iyaṟṟum aruṭkūttu uyirkaḷ uytaṟ poruṭṭēyaṉṟit taṉ poruṭṭu aṉ ṟeṉpatūum uṇaraṟpāṟṟu. iviyē ematu civacamaya vuṇmiyeṉṟu aṟivirāka!
urukutalic ceṉṟa uḷḷattum
	ampalattum oḷiyē
  perukutalic ceṉṟu niṉṟēṉ.	(tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyār, 104)

  kuṟiyoṉṟum illāta kūttaṉṟaṉ kūtti eṉakku
  aṟiyum vaṇṇam aruḷiyavāṟu ārpeṟuvār accōvē.				(accōppatikam, 2)
pattimiyum paricum ilāp pacupācam aṟuttaruḷip
  pittaṉ ivaṉeṉa eṉṉi ākkuvittup pērāmē
